            if R then
                R:Disconnect()
            end
            R = d.RenderStepped:Connect(function()

                if R and R.CameraSubject and R.CameraType == Enum.CameraType.Custom then
                    R.CFrame = R.CFrame
                end
            end)
        end
        f()

            task.wait(0.5)
            f()
        end)
    end
    local R = {}
    local d = {}
    local f = 0
    function _G.getSafePollRate()
        if os.clock() < f then
            return 0.27
        end
        return 0.1
    end
    function _G.triggerSafePollBoost()
        f = os.clock() + 3
    end
    local f = {}
    local function J()
        local a = n.Character
        return a and a:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    end
    local function n(a)
        for e, x in ipairs(r) do
            if
                a.X >= math.min(x.min.X, x.max.X)
                and a.X <= math.max(x.min.X, x.max.X)
                and a.Z >= math.min(x.min.Z, x.max.Z)
                and a.Z <= math.max(x.min.Z, x.max.Z)
            then
                return e
            end
        end
    end
    local function r(a)
        local e = a.Parent
        if not e then
            return
        end
        if e:IsA("Attachment") and e.Parent then
            e = e.Parent
        end
        if e:IsA("BasePart") then
            return e.Position
        elseif e:IsA("Model") then
            return e:GetPivot().Position
        end
    end
    local function a(e)
        if not P then
            return false
        end
        local x = P.SelectedPetData
        if not x then
            return false
        end
        local Q = e:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
        if not Q then
            return false
        end
        if x.plot then
            local V = e:FindFirstAncestor(x.plot)
            if not V then
                return false
            end
        end
        if x.slot then
            local V = e:FindFirstAncestor(x.slot)
            if V then
                return true
            end
            if Q.Name == x.slot then
                return true
            end
            if Q.Parent and Q.Parent.Name == x.slot then
                return true
            end
        end
        if x.name then
            local e = string.lower(x.name)
            local x = Q
            while x do
                if x.Name and string.lower(x.Name) == e then
                    return true
                end
                x = x.Parent
            end
        end
        return false
    end
    local function e(x, Q)
        if not x or not x.Parent then
            return false
        end
        if not x.Enabled then
            return false
        end
        local V = r(x)
        if not V then
            return false
        end
        local r = x:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
        if r then
            local r = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
            if r then
                local i = x:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("Model")
                while i and i.Parent ~= r do
                    i = i.Parent
                end
                if i then
                    local r = i:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
                    if r then
                        local i = r:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SurfaceGui", true)
                        local r = i and i:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel", true)
                        if r then
                            local i = r.Text:lower()
                            if
                                i:find(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Name:lower(), 1, true)
                                or i:find(game.Players.LocalPlayer.DisplayName:lower(), 1, true)
                            then
                                return false
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if _G.NEAREST_INSTANT_MODE == true then
            local r = n(Q)
            local i = n(V)
            if not r or r ~= i then
                return false
            end
        end
        if _G.NEAREST_INSTANT_MODE ~= true then
            if not a(x) then
                return false
            end
        end
        local r = typeof(x.MaxActivationDistance) == "number"
                and x.MaxActivationDistance > 0
                and x.MaxActivationDistance
            or C.RADIUS
        local n = math.min(C.RADIUS, r)
        return (V - Q).Magnitude <= n
    end
    local function r(n, a)
        local x = os.clock()
        local Q = d[n]
        if Q and x - Q < a then
            return false
        end
        d[n] = x
        return true
    end
    local function n(a, x, Q)
        if not a or not a.Parent then
            return
        end
        if not a.Enabled then
            return
        end
        if not r(a, Q) then
            return
        end
        for r = 1, x do
            pcall(function()
                fireproximityprompt(a, 0)
            end)
        end
    end
    local function r(a)
        if R[a] then
            return
        end
        R[a] = true
        local function x()
            local Q = J()
            if not Q then
                return
            end
            local V = Q.Position
            if e(a, V) then
                C.AUTO_STEAL = true
                local Q = os.clock()
                local V = f[a]
                if not V or Q - V >= 0.08 then
                    f[a] = Q
                    n(a, 25, 0)
                end
            end
        end
        task.defer(function()
            x()
        end)
        pcall(function()
            a:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Enabled"):Connect(function()
                if a.Enabled then
                    x()
                end
            end)
        end)
        a.AncestryChanged:Connect(function()
            if not a:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
                R[a] = nil
                d[a] = nil
                f[a] = nil
            end
        end)
    end
    local function d()
        local f = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        if not f then
            return
        end
        for a, a in ipairs(f:GetChildren()) do
            local f = a:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
            if f then
                for a, a in ipairs(f:GetDescendants()) do
                    if a:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
                        r(a)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    d()
    workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
        if d:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and d:FindFirstAncestor("AnimalPodiums") then
            r(d)
        end
    end)
    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait(_G.getSafePollRate()) do
            local r = J()
            if not r then
                C.AUTO_STEAL = false
                continue
            end
            local d = r.Position
            local r = false
            for f in pairs(R) do
                if e(f, d) then
                    r = true
                    if C.AUTO_STEAL then
                        n(f, 3, 0.12)
                    end
                end
            end
            C.AUTO_STEAL = r
        end
    end)
end

Q = setmetatable({}, {
    __index = function(V, V)
        local i = n.Packages.Net
        local O, U
        if V:sub(1, 3) == "RE/" then
            O = "RE/"
            U = V:sub(4)
        elseif V:sub(1, 3) == "RF/" then
            O = "RF/"
            U = V:sub(4)
        else
            return nil
        end
        local O
        for U, y in i:GetChildren() do
            if y.Name == V then
                O = i:GetChildren()[U + 1]
                break
            end
        end
        if O and not rawget(Q, V) then
            rawset(Q, V, O)
        end
        return rawget(Q, V)
    end,
})

function DeepCopy(i)
    if type(i) ~= "table" then
        return i
    end
    local O = {}
    for U, y in pairs(i) do
        O[U] = DeepCopy(y)
    end
    return O
end
function MergeDefaults(i, O)
    for U, y in pairs(O) do
        if type(y) == "table" then
            if type(i[U]) ~= "table" then
                i[U] = DeepCopy(y)
            else
                MergeDefaults(i[U], y)
            end
        elseif i[U] == nil then
            i[U] = y
        end
    end
end
local i = DeepCopy(V)
function NormalizeKeyName(O, U)
    if type(O) ~= "string" or O == "" then
        return U
    end
    local y = {
        ALT = "LeftAlt",
        LALT = "LeftAlt",
        RALT = "RightAlt",
        CTRL = "LeftControl",
        CONTROL = "LeftControl",
        LCTRL = "LeftControl",
        RCTRL = "RightControl",
        SHIFT = "LeftShift",
        LSHIFT = "LeftShift",
        RSHIFT = "RightShift",
        WIN = "LeftSuper",
        CMD = "LeftSuper",
        META = "LeftSuper",
    }
    local K = string.upper(O)
    local q = y[K] or O
    return Enum.KeyCode[q] and q or U
end
function PrettyKeyName(O)
    local U = {
        LeftAlt = "ALT",
        RightAlt = "RALT",
        LeftControl = "CTRL",
        RightControl = "RCTRL",
        LeftShift = "SHIFT",
        RightShift = "RSHIFT",
        LeftSuper = "WIN",
        RightSuper = "RWIN",
    }
    return U[O] or tostring(O or "")
end
if isfile and isfile("SlicedzHub.json") then
    pcall(function()
        local O = readfile("SlicedzHub.json")
        if not O or O == "" then
            return
        end
        local U = J:JSONDecode(O)
        if type(U) ~= "table" then
            return
        end
        local O = nil
        if type(U.PriorityList) == "table" then
            O = DeepCopy(U.PriorityList)
        end
        MergeDefaults(U, V)
        if O ~= nil then
            U.PriorityList = O
        end
        i = U
    end)
end

function EnsureSavedKeybinds()
    i.SavedKeybinds = i.SavedKeybinds or {}
    i.SavedKeybinds.MenuKey =
        NormalizeKeyName(i.SavedKeybinds.MenuKey or i.MenuKey, V.MenuKey or "LeftControl")
    i.SavedKeybinds.ClickToAPKeybind =
        NormalizeKeyName(i.SavedKeybinds.ClickToAPKeybind or i.ClickToAPKeybind, V.ClickToAPKeybind or "L")
    i.SavedKeybinds.ProximityAPKeybind = NormalizeKeyName(
        i.SavedKeybinds.ProximityAPKeybind or i.ProximityAPKeybind,
        V.ProximityAPKeybind or "P"
    )
    i.SavedKeybinds.StealSpeedKey =
        NormalizeKeyName(i.SavedKeybinds.StealSpeedKey or i.StealSpeedKey, V.StealSpeedKey or "C")
    i.SavedKeybinds.FloatKeybind =
        NormalizeKeyName(i.SavedKeybinds.FloatKeybind or i.FloatKeybind, V.FloatKeybind or "Z")
    i.SavedKeybinds.ResetKey = NormalizeKeyName(i.SavedKeybinds.ResetKey or i.ResetKey, V.ResetKey or "X")
    i.SavedKeybinds.JobJoinerKey =
        NormalizeKeyName(i.SavedKeybinds.JobJoinerKey or i.JobJoinerKey, V.JobJoinerKey or "J")
    i.SavedKeybinds.InvisToggleKey =
        NormalizeKeyName(i.SavedKeybinds.InvisToggleKey or i.InvisToggleKey, V.InvisToggleKey or "I")
    i.SavedKeybinds.KickKey = NormalizeKeyName(i.SavedKeybinds.KickKey or i.KickKey, "")
    i.SavedKeybinds.RagdollSelfKey = NormalizeKeyName(i.SavedKeybinds.RagdollSelfKey or i.RagdollSelfKey, "B")
    i.SavedKeybinds.TpKey =
        NormalizeKeyName(i.SavedKeybinds.TpKey or i.TpSettings.TpKey, V.TpSettings.TpKey or "T")
    i.SavedKeybinds.CloneKey =
        NormalizeKeyName(i.SavedKeybinds.CloneKey or i.TpSettings.CloneKey, V.TpSettings.CloneKey or "V")
    i.SavedKeybinds.CarpetSpeedKey = NormalizeKeyName(
        i.SavedKeybinds.CarpetSpeedKey or i.TpSettings.CarpetSpeedKey,
        V.TpSettings.CarpetSpeedKey or "Q"
    )
end
function ApplySavedKeybindsToConfig()
    EnsureSavedKeybinds()
    i.MenuKey = i.SavedKeybinds.MenuKey
    i.ClickToAPKeybind = i.SavedKeybinds.ClickToAPKeybind
    i.ProximityAPKeybind = i.SavedKeybinds.ProximityAPKeybind
    i.StealSpeedKey = i.SavedKeybinds.StealSpeedKey
    i.FloatKeybind = i.SavedKeybinds.FloatKeybind
    i.ResetKey = i.SavedKeybinds.ResetKey
    i.JobJoinerKey = i.SavedKeybinds.JobJoinerKey
    i.InvisToggleKey = i.SavedKeybinds.InvisToggleKey
    i.KickKey = i.SavedKeybinds.KickKey
    i.RagdollSelfKey = i.SavedKeybinds.RagdollSelfKey
    i.TpSettings.TpKey = i.SavedKeybinds.TpKey
    i.TpSettings.CloneKey = i.SavedKeybinds.CloneKey
    i.TpSettings.CarpetSpeedKey = i.SavedKeybinds.CarpetSpeedKey
end
local function O(U)
    local y = { [1] = 0.1, [2] = 0.2, [3] = 0.3, [4] = 0.4 }
    return y[tonumber(U)] or 0.1
end
function NormalizeAllKeybinds()
    EnsureSavedKeybinds()
    ApplySavedKeybindsToConfig()
end

end

local function z(B)
    local o = C.Character
    local Y = o:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
    local T = o:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local o = q()
    local q = T.CFrame.LookVector
    o:Disable()
    local T = os.clock()
    local I
    I = R.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if os.clock() - T >= B then
            I:Disconnect()
            Y:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
            o:Enable()
            return
        end
        Y:Move(q, false)
    end)
end
local function q()
    local B = game:GetService("Players")
    local o = B.LocalPlayer
    if not o then
        return
    end
    local B = o.Character or o.CharacterAdded:Wait()
    local Y = B:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not Y then
        return
    end
    local T = o.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Quantum Cloner") or B:FindFirstChild("Quantum Cloner")
    if not T then
        return
    end
    if T.Parent ~= B then
        Y:EquipTool(T)
        task.wait()
    end
    local B = o:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
    local o = B:FindFirstChild("ToolsFrames")
    local B = o and o:FindFirstChild("QuantumCloner")
    local o = B and B:FindFirstChild("TeleportToClone")
    if not o then
        return
    end
    T:Activate()
    task.wait(0.05)
    o.Visible = true
    if typeof(firesignal) == "function" then
        firesignal(o.MouseButton1Up)
        if _G.triggerSafePollBoost then
            _G.triggerSafePollBoost()
        end
    end
end
local function B(o)
    local Y = 0
    while Y < o do
        Y += R.Heartbeat:Wait()
    end
end
local function o(Y, T)
    local I = 0
    while true do
        if Y() then
            return true
        end
        local Y = R.Heartbeat:Wait()
        I += Y
        if T and I >= T then
            return false
        end
    end
end
local function Y(T)
    if not T or not T:IsA("BasePart") then
        return nil
    end
    local I = nil
    local p = math.huge
    for j, j in ipairs(a:GetDescendants()) do
        if j:IsA("TextLabel") then
            local l = tostring(j.Text or "")
            if l ~= "" and l:lower():find("base", 1, true) then
                local l = j:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("SurfaceGui")
                if l then
                    local j = l.Adornee or l.Parent
                    if j and j:IsA("BasePart") then
                        local l = (j.Position - T.Position).Magnitude
                        if l < p then
                            p = l
                            I = j
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return I
end
local function T(I, p)
    if not I then
        return
    end
    local j = os.clock()
    while I.Parent and I.Position.Y < p do
        local l = p - I.Position.Y
        local p = math.clamp(l * 20, 280, 310)
        I.Velocity = Vector3.new(I.Velocity.X, p, I.Velocity.Z)
        if os.clock() - j > 3 then
            break
        end
        R.Heartbeat:Wait()
    end
    I.Velocity = Vector3.new(I.Velocity.X, 0, I.Velocity.Z)
end
local function I(p)
    if not p then
        return nil
    end
    local j = p.Parent
    local l = i.TpSettings.Tool or "Flying Carpet"
    local W = C.Backpack:FindFirstChild(l) or j and j:FindFirstChild(l)
    if W then
        if W.Parent ~= j then
            p:EquipTool(W)
        else
            p:EquipTool(W)
        end
        task.wait(0.02)
    end
    return W
end
local function p(j, l)
    if not j or not l then
        return
    end
    local l = j.Parent
    local j = i.TpSettings.Tool or "Flying Carpet"
    local W = C.Backpack:FindFirstChild(j) or l and l:FindFirstChild(j)
    if not W then
        return
    end
end
local j = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local l = {
    { name = "MED1", pos = Vector3.new(-410.65, -5.68, -46.1) },
    { name = "MED2", pos = Vector3.new(-410.91, -5.68, 168.89) },
}
local W = {
    { name = "TP1", pos = Vector3.new(-488.88, 15, 196.38), facing = "back" },
    { name = "TP2", pos = Vector3.new(-487.79, 15, 138.13), facing = "front" },
    { name = "TP3", pos = Vector3.new(-489.38, 15, 89.23), facing = "back" },
    { name = "TP4", pos = Vector3.new(-489.69, 15, 30.98), facing = "front" },
    { name = "TP5", pos = Vector3.new(-488.75, 15, -17.95), facing = "back" },
    { name = "TP6", pos = Vector3.new(-490.0, 15, -75.9), facing = "front" },
    { name = "TP7", pos = Vector3.new(-331.75, 15, -75.8), facing = "back" },
    { name = "TP8", pos = Vector3.new(-329.98, 15, -18.16), facing = "front" },
    { name = "TP9", pos = Vector3.new(-330.04, 15, 31.14), facing = "back" },
    { name = "TP10", pos = Vector3.new(-331.28, 15, 88.92), facing = "front" },
    { name = "TP11", pos = Vector3.new(-330.57, 15, 138.1), facing = "back" },
    { name = "TP12", pos = Vector3.new(-330.01, 15, 195.96), facing = "front" },
}
local function X(t, G)
    return (Vector3.new(t.X, 0, t.Z) - Vector3.new(G.X, 0, G.Z)).Magnitude
end
local function t(G)
    local v = nil
    local M = math.huge
    for w, w in ipairs(a:GetDescendants()) do
        if w:IsA("TextLabel") then
            local b = tostring(w.Text or "")
            if b ~= "" and b:lower():find("base", 1, true) then
                local b = w:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("SurfaceGui")
                if b then
                    local w = b.Adornee or b.Parent
                    if w and w:IsA("BasePart") then
                        local b = (w.Position - G).Magnitude
                        if b < M then
                            M = b
                            v = w
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return v
end
local function G(v)
    local M = nil
    local w = math.huge
    for b, b in ipairs(l) do
        local l = X(v, b.pos)
        if l < w then
            w = l
            M = b
        end
    end
    return M, w
end
local l = {
    MED1 = { TP6 = true, TP7 = true, TP8 = true, TP10 = true, TP12 = true, TP5 = true, TP3 = true, TP1 = true },
    MED2 = { TP1 = true, TP2 = true, TP4 = true, TP6 = true, TP7 = true, TP9 = true, TP11 = true, TP12 = true },
}
local function v(M, w)
    local b = M and M.pos
    local D = M and M.name
    if not b or not D then
        return nil
    end
    local M = l[D]
    if not M then
        return nil
    end
    local l = nil
    local D = math.huge
    local F = math.huge
    for E, E in ipairs(W) do
        if M[E.name] then
            local W = X(b, E.pos)
            local M = X(w, E.pos)
            if M < F or math.abs(M - F) <= 0.001 and W < D then
                l = E
                D = W
                F = M
            end
        end
    end
    return l, D, F
end
local function l(W, M, w)
    if not W or not M or not w then
        return false, "missing target/hum/hrp"
    end
    local b = w.Position
    local D = W.Position
    local D = G(b)
    if not D then
        return false, "no MED point found"
    end
    local b = W.Position
    local F = v(D, b)
    if not F then
        return false, "no TP1-TP12 point found"
    end
    local v = b.Y <= 6.313370704650879
    local b = v and Vector3.new(F.pos.X, 1, F.pos.Z) or F.pos
    local v = t(b) or Y(W)
    local W = nil
    if v then
        W = F.facing == "front" and v.CFrame.LookVector or -v.CFrame.LookVector
        if W.Magnitude <= 0 then
            W = nil
        end
    end
    local t = a.CurrentCamera
    local v = t and t.CameraType
    local E = t and t.FieldOfView
    local m = nil
    if W then
        w.Velocity = Vector3.zero
        w.CFrame = CFrame.new(w.Position, w.Position + W)
        w.Velocity = Vector3.zero
    end
    if t then
        t.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
        t.FieldOfView = 95
        m = R.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
            local A = C.Character and C.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if A and t then
                t.CFrame = A.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 2, 10) * CFrame.Angles(-0.08726646259971647, 0, 0)
            end
        end)
    end
    local function A()
        if m then
            m:Disconnect()
            m = nil
        end
        if t then
            if v then
                t.CameraType = v
            end
            if E then
                t.FieldOfView = E
            end
        end
    end
    local function t(v, E)
        A()
        return v, E
    end
    w.Velocity = Vector3.zero
    if M and M.Parent then
        M:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
    end
    task.wait(0.01)
    if not w or not w.Parent then
        return t(false, "hrp lost during jump start")
    end
    local v = Vector3.new(D.pos.X, w.Position.Y, D.pos.Z)
    w.Velocity = Vector3.zero
    w.CFrame = W and CFrame.new(v, v + W) or CFrame.new(v)
    w.Velocity = Vector3.zero
    task.wait(0.1)
    if not w or not w.Parent then
        return t(false, "hrp lost after MED reposition")
    end
    local v = o(function()
        return w and w.Parent and X(w.Position, D.pos) <= 2.5
    end, 1.5)
    if not v then
        return t(false, "failed to reach MED point")
    end
    T(w, 45)
    w.Velocity = Vector3.zero
    w.CFrame = W and CFrame.new(b, b + W) or CFrame.new(b)
    w.Velocity = Vector3.zero
    local W = o(function()
        return w and w.Parent and (w.Position - b).Magnitude <= 2.5
    end, 3)
    if not W then
        return t(false, "failed to reach TP1-TP12")
    end
    B(0.1)
    B(0.05)
    local W = o(function()
        return w and w.Parent and (w.Position - b).Magnitude <= 2.5
    end, 0.75)
    if not W then
        return t(false, "moved away from TP1-TP12 before clone")
    end
    w.Velocity = Vector3.zero
    w.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
    local W = w.Position
    task.wait(0.1)
    q()
    local X = o(function()
        return w and w.Parent and (w.Position - W).Magnitude >= 0.5
    end, 5)
    while _G.isCloning do
        task.wait()
    end
    task.wait(0.2)
    return t(true, F.name)
end
local W = game:GetService("CoreGui")
local X = nil
local t = nil
local v = nil
local M = nil
local w = nil
local function b(D, F)
    pcall(function()
        if writefile then
            writefile(
                "HazeButtonsClean2_Pos.json",
                J:JSONEncode({
                    xScale = D.X.Scale,
                    xOffset = D.X.Offset,
                    yScale = D.Y.Scale,
                    yOffset = D.Y.Offset,
                    locked = F,
                })
            )
        end
    end)
end
local function D()
    local F = UDim2.new(0.5, -108.0, 0.5, -21.0)
    local E, m = pcall(function()
        if readfile and isfile and isfile("HazeButtonsClean2_Pos.json") then
            return J:JSONDecode(readfile("HazeButtonsClean2_Pos.json"))
        end
    end)
    if E and m then
        return UDim2.new(m.xScale or 0.5, m.xOffset or -108.0, m.yScale or 0.5, m.yOffset or -21.0),
            m.locked == true
    end
    return F, false
end
local function J()
    local F = C.Character
    if not F then
        return
    end
    return F:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or F:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
end
local function F(E)
    local m = C.Character
    local A = J()
    if not m or not A then
        return
    end
    local m = a:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    if not m then
        return
    end
    local H, _ = nil, 40
    for Z, Z in pairs(m:GetChildren()) do
        local m
        if Z:IsA("Model") then
            m = Z.PrimaryPart and Z.PrimaryPart.Position or Z:GetPivot().Position
        else
            m = Z.Position
        end
        local k = (A.Position - m).Magnitude
        if k < _ then
            H = Z
            _ = k
        end
    end
    if H and H:FindFirstChild("Unlock") then
        local m = {}
        for A, A in pairs(H.Unlock:GetChildren()) do
            local H = A:IsA("Model") and A:GetPivot().Position or A.Position
            table.insert(m, { Obj = A, Y = H.Y })
        end
        table.sort(m, function(A, H)
            return A.Y < H.Y
        end)
        if m[E] then
            for A, A in pairs(m[E].Obj:GetDescendants()) do
                if A:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
                    pcall(function()
                        fireproximityprompt(A)
                    end)
                end
            end
        end
    end
end
local function E()
    local m = J()
    if not m then
        return 1
    end
    local J = m.Position.Y
    if J < 12 then
        return 1
    elseif J < 30 then
        return 2
    else
        return 3
    end
end
local function J()
    local m = E()
    F(m)
    ShowNotification("AUTO UNLOCK", "Level " .. tostring(m))
end
local function E()
    if X then
        pcall(function()
            X:Destroy()
        end)
    end
    X = nil
    t = nil
    v = nil
    M = nil
    w = nil
end
function toggleUnlockBaseMenu(m)
    if not m then
        E()
        return
    end
    E()
    local E, m = D()
    local function D(A, H)
        local _ = Instance.new("UICorner")
        _.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, H)
        _.Parent = A
        return _
    end
    local function A(H, _, Z, k)
        local u = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        u.Color = _
        u.Thickness = Z or 1
        u.Transparency = k or 0
        u.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
        u.Parent = H
        return u
    end
    local function H(_, Z, k, u, g)
        f:Create(_, TweenInfo.new(Z or 0.16, u or Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, g or Enum.EasingDirection.Out), k)
            :Play()
    end
    local function _(Z, k, u, g)
        local h = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        h.Color = ColorSequence.new({ ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, k), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, u) })
        h.Rotation = g or 0
        h.Parent = Z
        return h
    end
    local Z = {
        BG = Color3.fromRGB(22, 10, 10),

    v.BorderSizePixel = 0

    v.Parent = X
    D(v, 10)

    local function E()

            v.BackgroundColor3 = Z.LOCK_RED_1
            M.Color = ColorSequence.new({

    j.InputChanged:Connect(function(M)
        if m then

        end
        if X and M.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
            local X = M.Position - t
            W.Position = UDim2.new(v.X.Scale, v.X.Offset + X.X, v.Y.Scale, v.Y.Offset + X.Y)
        end

end

    Background = Color3.fromRGB(18, 8, 8),

local X = {}
local function t()
    for v = #X, 1, -1.0 do
        X[v] = nil
    end
    for v, M in ipairs(i.PriorityList or {}) do
        X[v] = M
    end
end
local function v()
    i.PriorityList = {}
    for M, w in ipairs(X) do
        i.PriorityList[M] = w
    end
end
t()
local function M(w)
    if not w then
        return nil
    end
    local b = a:FindFirstChild("Plots") and a.Plots:FindFirstChild(w.plot)
    if b then
        local D = b:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
        if D then
            local b = D:FindFirstChild(w.slot)
            if b then
                local w = b:FindFirstChild("Base")
                if w then
                    local b = w:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
                    if b then
                        return b
                    end
                    return w:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart") or w
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end
local function w(b)
    if not b then
        return
    end
    if not d then
        return
    end
    local D = b:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale")
    if D then
        D:Destroy()
    end
    local D = Instance.new("UIScale")
    D.Parent = b
    P.MobileScaleObjects[b] = D
    if P.RefreshMobileScale then
        P.RefreshMobileScale()
    else
        D.Scale = math.clamp(tonumber(i.MobileGuiScale) or 0.5, 0, 1)
    end
end
P.RefreshMobileScale = function()
    local b = math.clamp(tonumber(i.MobileGuiScale) or 0.5, 0, 1)
    for D, F in pairs(P.MobileScaleObjects) do
        if D and D.Parent and F and F.Parent == D then
            F.Scale = b
        else
            P.MobileScaleObjects[D] = nil
        end
    end
end
local function b(D, F)
    if not d then
        return
    end
    if not D or not D.Parent then
        return
    end
    local E = D.Parent
    local m = D:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Frame")
    if not m then
        return
    end

local function D(F, E, m)
    local A, H, _, Z
    F.InputBegan:Connect(function(k)
        if i.UILocked then
            return
        end
        if _G.ADMIN_PANEL_SLIDER_DRAG then
            return
        end
        if
            k.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            or k.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
        then
            A = true
            _ = k.Position
            Z = E.Position
            k.Changed:Connect(function()
                if k.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                    A = false
                    if m then
                        i.Positions[m] = {
                            X = E.Position.X.Scale,
                            Y = E.Position.Y.Scale,
                            OffsetX = E.Position.X.Offset,
                            OffsetY = E.Position.Y.Offset,
                        }
                        U()
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
    F.InputChanged:Connect(function(F)
        if
            F.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
            or F.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
        then
            H = F
        end
    end)
    j.InputChanged:Connect(function(F)
        if _G.ADMIN_PANEL_SLIDER_DRAG then
            return
        end
        if F == H and A then
            local m = F.Position - _
            E.Position = UDim2.new(Z.X.Scale, Z.X.Offset + m.X, Z.Y.Scale, Z.Y.Offset + m.Y)
        end
    end)
end

local function E(m)
    return r:GetPlayerFromCharacter(m) ~= nil
end
local function m(A)
    local H = A:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
    if H and E(H) then
        return
    end
    for H, H in pairs(A:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
        H:Stop(0)
    end
    A.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(A)
        A:Stop(0)
    end)
end
local A = nil
local function H(_)
    local Z = _:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
    local k = Z and E(Z)
    if _:IsA("Animator") then
        m(_)
    end
    if _:IsA("Accessory") or _:IsA("Clothing") then
        if _:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model") then
            _:Destroy()
        end
    end
    if not k then
        if
            _:IsA("ParticleEmitter")
            or _:IsA("Trail")
            or _:IsA("Beam")
            or _:IsA("Smoke")
            or _:IsA("Fire")
            or _:IsA("Sparkles")
            or _:IsA("Highlight")
        then
            _.Enabled = false
        end
        if _:IsA("PointLight") or _:IsA("SpotLight") or _:IsA("SurfaceLight") then
            _.Enabled = false
            _.Brightness = 0
            _.Range = 0
            task.defer(function()
                pcall(function()
                    _:Destroy()
                end)
            end)
        end
        if _:IsA("Explosion") then
            _:Destroy()
        end
        if _:IsA("MeshPart") then
            _.TextureID = ""
        end
    end
    if _:IsA("BasePart") then
        _.Material = Enum.Material.Plastic
        _.Reflectance = 0
        _.CastShadow = false
        pcall(function()
            if _.Material == Enum.Material.Glass or _.Material == Enum.Material.Neon then
                _.Material = Enum.Material.Plastic
            end
        end)
    end
    if _:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") or _:IsA("Texture") or _:IsA("Decal") then
        _:Destroy()
    end
end
local _ = game:GetService("Lighting")
getgenv().DayLock = getgenv().DayLock or { Enabled = false, Hour = 7, Refresh = 0.1 }
if getgenv().DayLock.Enabled == nil then
    getgenv().DayLock.Enabled = false
end
if type(getgenv().DayLock.Hour) ~= "number" then
    getgenv().DayLock.Hour = 7
end
if type(getgenv().DayLock.Refresh) ~= "number" then
    getgenv().DayLock.Refresh = 0.1
end
task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        local Z = getgenv().DayLock
        if Z and Z.Enabled then
            _.ClockTime = Z.Hour
            _.TimeOfDay = string.format("%02d:00:00", Z.Hour)
        end
        task.wait(Z and Z.Refresh or 0.1)
    end
end)
local function Z(k)
    i.FPSBoost = k
    U()
    if A then
        A:Disconnect()
        A = nil
    end
    getgenv().DayLock = getgenv().DayLock or {}
    getgenv().DayLock.Hour = 7
    getgenv().DayLock.Refresh = 0.1
    getgenv().DayLock.Enabled = k and true or false
    if k then
        _.ClockTime = getgenv().DayLock.Hour
        _.TimeOfDay = string.format("%02d:00:00", getgenv().DayLock.Hour)
        task.spawn(function()
            local k = a:GetDescendants()
            for u = 1, #k do
                H(k[u])
                if u % 30 == 0 then
                    task.wait()
                end
            end
        end)
        A = a.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(A)
            if not i.FPSBoost then
                return
            end
            task.defer(function()
                H(A)
            end)
        end)
        local A = a:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")
        pcall(function()
            _.Brightness = 2
            _.GlobalShadows = false
            _.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(165, 165, 165)
            _.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20)
            _.FogEnd = 9000000000
            _.FogStart = 0
            _.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = 0
            _.EnvironmentSpecularScale = 0
            for H, H in pairs(_:GetChildren()) do
                if
                    H:IsA("BlurEffect")
                    or H:IsA("SunRaysEffect")
                    or H:IsA("BloomEffect")
                    or H:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect")
                    or H:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect")
                then
                    H:Destroy()
                end
            end
            if A then
                A.WaterWaveSize = 0
                A.WaterWaveSpeed = 0
                A.WaterReflectance = 0
                A.WaterTransparency = 1
            end
        end)
    end
end
if i.FPSBoost then
    task.spawn(function()
        task.wait(1)
        Z(true)
    end)
end
local A = nil
local function H(Z, k)
    local u = Z:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
    local g = u and E(u)
    if Z:IsA("Animator") then
        m(Z)
    end
    if Z:IsA("Accessory") or Z:IsA("Clothing") then
        if Z:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model") then
            Z:Destroy()
        end
    end
    if not g then
        if
            Z:IsA("ParticleEmitter")
            or Z:IsA("Trail")
            or Z:IsA("Beam")
            or Z:IsA("Smoke")
            or Z:IsA("Fire")
            or Z:IsA("Sparkles")
            or Z:IsA("Highlight")
        then
            Z.Enabled = false
        end
        if Z:IsA("Explosion") then
            Z:Destroy()
        end
        if Z:IsA("MeshPart") then
            Z.TextureID = ""
        end
        if k and (Z:IsA("PointLight") or Z:IsA("SpotLight") or Z:IsA("SurfaceLight")) then
            pcall(function()
                if Z.Parent ~= _ then
                    Z.Enabled = false
                    Z.Brightness = 0
                    Z.Range = 0
                    Z.Shadows = false
                    Z:Destroy()
                end
            end)
        end
    end
    if Z:IsA("BasePart") then
        Z.Material = Enum.Material.Plastic
        Z.Reflectance = 0
        Z.CastShadow = false
    end
    if Z:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") or Z:IsA("Texture") or Z:IsA("Decal") then
        Z:Destroy()
    end
end
local function E(m)
    i.FPSBoostV2 = m
    U()
    if A then
        A:Disconnect()
        A = nil
    end
    if m then
        _.GlobalShadows = false
        _.FogEnd = 1000000
        _.FogStart = 0
        _.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = 0
        _.EnvironmentSpecularScale = 0.1
        for m, m in pairs(_:GetChildren()) do
            if
                m:IsA("BloomEffect")
                or m:IsA("BlurEffect")
                or m:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect")
                or m:IsA("SunRaysEffect")
                or m:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect")
                or m:IsA("Atmosphere")
            then
                m:Destroy()
            end
        end
        task.spawn(function()
            local m = a:GetDescendants()
            for Z = 1, #m do
                H(m[Z], true)
                if Z % 30 == 0 then
                    task.wait()
                end
            end
        end)
        A = a.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(m)
            if not i.FPSBoostV2 then
                return
            end
            task.defer(function()
                H(m, false)
            end)
        end)
    end
end
if i.FPSBoostV2 then
    task.spawn(function()
        task.wait(1)
        E(true)
    end)
end

    { carpetSpeedConnection = nil, infiniteJumpConnection = nil, antiRagdollConn = nil, antiRagdollV2Task = nil }

local k = A.carpetSpeedConnection
local u = H.carpetStatusLabel
local g = Vector3.new(7, 1, 7)
local h = { platform = nil, followConnection = nil, sawStealingDuringThisFloat = false }
local L = {}
local function s()
    local S = C.Character
    if not S then
        return nil, nil, nil
    end
    local N = S:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local PG = S:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    return S, N, PG
end
local function S()
    if h.followConnection then
        h.followConnection:Disconnect()
        h.followConnection = nil
    end
    if h.platform then
        h.platform:Destroy()
        h.platform = nil
    end
end
local function N()
    local PG, PG, CG = s()
    if not PG or not CG or not h.platform then
        return
    end
    if CG.Health <= 0 then
        return
    end
    local CG = PG.Position - Vector3.new(0, 3.35, 0)
    if (h.platform.Position - CG).Magnitude > 0.05 then
        h.platform.CFrame = CFrame.new(CG)
    end
end
local function PG()
    S()
    local CG, CG, rG = s()
    if not CG or not rG then
        return
    end
    local rG = Instance.new("Part")
    rG.Name = "HAZE_FLOAT_PLATFORM"
    rG.Size = g
    rG.Anchored = true
    rG.CanCollide = true
    rG.CanTouch = false
    rG.CanQuery = false
    rG.Transparency = 1
    rG.CastShadow = false
    rG.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
    rG.Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    rG.TopSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
    rG.BottomSurface = Enum.SurfaceType.Smooth
    rG.Locked = true
    rG.Massless = true
    rG.CFrame = CFrame.new(CG.Position - Vector3.new(0, 3.35, 0))
    rG.Parent = a
    h.platform = rG
    h.followConnection = R.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not m.floatActive then
            return
        end
        local g, CG, rG = s()
        if not g or not CG or not rG or rG.Health <= 0 then
            return
        end
        N()
    end)
    N()
end
local function g()
    if not m.floatActive then
        h.sawStealingDuringThisFloat = false
        return
    end
    h.sawStealingDuringThisFloat = (C:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true)
end
local function s(N, CG)
    N = not not N
    m.floatActive = N
    i.Float = N
    if N then
        g()
        PG()
    else
        S()
        h.sawStealingDuringThisFloat = false
    end
    U()
    if L and L.setFn then
        pcall(L.setFn, N)
    end
    if not CG and type(F) == "function" then
        F("FLOAT", N and "ENABLED" or "DISABLED")
    end
end
local function N(CG)
    s(not m.floatActive, CG)
end
_G.setFloatEnabled = s
_G.toggleFloat = N
C:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Stealing"):Connect(function()
    local CG = C:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true
    if not m.floatActive then
        return
    end
    if CG then
        h.sawStealingDuringThisFloat = true
        return
    end
    if h.sawStealingDuringThisFloat then
        s(false, false)
        if type(F) == "function" then
            F("FLOAT", "Disabled")
        end
    end
end)
C.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    task.wait(0.35)

        g()
        PG()
    end
end)

        g()

    end
end)
local function g(h)
    m.carpetSpeedEnabled = h
    Z = m.carpetSpeedEnabled
    if A.carpetSpeedConnection then
        A.carpetSpeedConnection:Disconnect()
        A.carpetSpeedConnection = nil
    end
    k = A.carpetSpeedConnection
    if not h then
        return
    end
    if P.DisableStealSpeed then
        P.DisableStealSpeed()
    end
    A.carpetSpeedConnection = R.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        k = A.carpetSpeedConnection
        local k = C.Character
        if not k then
            return
        end
        local h = k:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
        local S = k:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not h or not S then
            return
        end
        local PG = i.TpSettings.Tool
        local CG = k:FindFirstChild(PG)
        if not CG then
            local k = C.Backpack:FindFirstChild(PG)
            if k then
                h:EquipTool(k)
            end
        end
        if CG then
            local k = h.MoveDirection
            if k.Magnitude > 0 then
                S.Velocity = Vector3.new(k.X * 140, S.Velocity.Y, k.Z * 140)
            else
                S.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, S.Velocity.Y, 0)
            end
        end
    end)
end
local k = { lastJumpTime = 0 }
local h = m.infiniteJumpEnabled
local S = A.infiniteJumpConnection
local function PG(CG)
    m.infiniteJumpEnabled = CG
    h = m.infiniteJumpEnabled
    i.TpSettings.InfiniteJump = CG
    U()
    if A.infiniteJumpConnection then
        A.infiniteJumpConnection:Disconnect()
        A.infiniteJumpConnection = nil
    end
    if A.infiniteJumpBtnBegin then
        A.infiniteJumpBtnBegin:Disconnect()
        A.infiniteJumpBtnBegin = nil
    end
    if A.infiniteJumpBtnEnd then
        A.infiniteJumpBtnEnd:Disconnect()
        A.infiniteJumpBtnEnd = nil
    end
    if A.infiniteJumpCharConn then
        A.infiniteJumpCharConn:Disconnect()
        A.infiniteJumpCharConn = nil
    end
    _ijMobileHeld = false
    S = A.infiniteJumpConnection
    if not CG then
        return
    end
    local CG = false
    local function rG()
        local RG = C:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        if not RG then
            return
        end
        local dG = RG:FindFirstChild("TouchGui")
        if not dG then
            return
        end
        local RG = dG:FindFirstChild("TouchControlFrame")
        if not RG then
            return
        end
        local dG = RG:FindFirstChild("JumpButton")
        if not dG then
            return
        end
        if A.infiniteJumpBtnBegin then
            A.infiniteJumpBtnBegin:Disconnect()
        end
        if A.infiniteJumpBtnEnd then
            A.infiniteJumpBtnEnd:Disconnect()
        end
        A.infiniteJumpBtnBegin = dG.InputBegan:Connect(function(RG)
            if RG.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                CG = true
            end
        end)
        A.infiniteJumpBtnEnd = dG.InputEnded:Connect(function(RG)
            if RG.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                CG = false
            end
        end)
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        for RG = 1, 10 do
            local RG = C:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
            local dG = RG and RG:FindFirstChild("TouchGui")
            if
                dG
                and dG:FindFirstChild("TouchControlFrame")
                and dG.TouchControlFrame:FindFirstChild("JumpButton")
            then
                rG()
                break
            end
            task.wait(0.5)
        end
    end)
    A.infiniteJumpCharConn = C.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        CG = false
        task.delay(1, rG)
    end)
    A.infiniteJumpConnection = R.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        S = A.infiniteJumpConnection
        if not j:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) and not CG then
            return
        end
        local S = tick()
        if S - k.lastJumpTime < 0.1 then
            return
        end
        local CG = C.Character
        if not CG then
            return
        end
        local rG = CG:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local RG = CG:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
        if not rG or not RG or RG.Health <= 0 then
            return
        end
        k.lastJumpTime = S
        rG.Velocity = Vector3.new(rG.Velocity.X, 55, rG.Velocity.Z)
    end)
end
if h then
    PG(true)

local k = m.antiRagdollMode
local S = A.antiRagdollConn
local function CG()
    local rG = C.Character
    if not rG then
        return false
    end
    local RG = rG:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not RG then
        return false
    end
    local rG = RG:GetState()
    local RG = {
        [Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics] = true,
        [Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll] = true,
        [Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown] = true,
    }
    if RG[rG] then
        return true
    end
    local rG = C:GetAttribute("RagdollEndTime")
    if rG and rG - a:GetServerTimeNow() > 0 then
        return true
    end
    return false
end
local function rG()
    if A.antiRagdollConn then
        A.antiRagdollConn:Disconnect()
        A.antiRagdollConn = nil
    end
    S = A.antiRagdollConn
end
local function RG(dG)
    rG()
    if i.AntiRagdollV2 then
        stopAntiRagdollV2()
    end
    if dG == 0 then
        return
    end
    A.antiRagdollConn = R.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        S = A.antiRagdollConn
        local A = C.Character
        if not A then
            return
        end
        local S = A:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local dG = A:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not S or not dG then
            return
        end
        if CG() then
            pcall(function()
                C:SetAttribute("RagdollEndTime", a:GetServerTimeNow())
            end)
            S:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
            dG.Velocity = Vector3.zero
            if a.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject ~= S then
                a.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = S
            end
            for S, S in ipairs(A:GetDescendants()) do
                if S:IsA("BallSocketConstraint") or S.Name:find("RagdollAttachment") then
                    pcall(function()
                        S:Destroy()
                    end)
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end
local A = { antiRagdollConns = {} }
local S = A.antiRagdollConns
local CG = false
local function dG(nG)
    if not nG then
        return
    end
    local fG = false
    pcall(function()
        local JG = i.TpSettings.Tool or "Flying Carpet"
        local aG = nG:FindFirstChild(JG)
        if aG then
            local JG = nG:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if JG then
                for eG, eG in ipairs(JG:GetChildren()) do
                    if eG:IsA("BodyVelocity") or eG:IsA("BodyPosition") or eG:IsA("BodyGyro") then
                        fG = true
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
            if not fG then
                for JG, JG in ipairs(aG:GetChildren()) do
                    if JG:IsA("BodyVelocity") or JG:IsA("BodyPosition") or JG:IsA("BodyGyro") then
                        fG = true
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    local JG = nG:GetDescendants()
    for aG, aG in ipairs(JG) do
        if
            aG:IsA("BallSocketConstraint")
            or aG:IsA("NoCollisionConstraint")
            or aG:IsA("HingeConstraint")
            or aG:IsA("Attachment") and (aG.Name == "A" or aG.Name == "B")
        then
            aG:Destroy()
        elseif (aG:IsA("BodyVelocity") or aG:IsA("BodyPosition") or aG:IsA("BodyGyro")) and not fG then
            aG:Destroy()
        end
    end
    for fG, fG in ipairs(JG) do
        if fG:IsA("Motor6D") then
            fG.Enabled = true
        end
    end
    local fG = nG:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if fG then
        local nG = fG:FindFirstChild("Animator")
        if nG then
            for fG, fG in ipairs(nG:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                local nG = fG.Animation and fG.Animation.Name:lower() or ""
                if nG:find("rag") or nG:find("fall") or nG:find("hurt") or nG:find("down") then
                    fG:Stop(0)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    task.defer(function()
        pcall(function()
            local nG = C:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts")
            if nG then
                nG = nG:FindFirstChild("PlayerModule")
            end
            if nG then
                require(nG):GetControls():Enable()
            end
        end)
    end)
end
local function nG(fG)
    if CG then
        return
    end
    CG = true
    task.defer(function()
        CG = false
        if fG and fG.Parent then
            dG(fG)
        end
    end)
end
local function fG(JG)
    if JG:IsA("BallSocketConstraint") or JG:IsA("NoCollisionConstraint") or JG:IsA("HingeConstraint") then
        return true
    end
    if JG:IsA("Attachment") and (JG.Name == "A" or JG.Name == "B") then
        return true
    end
    if JG:IsA("BodyVelocity") or JG:IsA("BodyPosition") or JG:IsA("BodyGyro") then
        return true
    end
    return false
end
local function JG(aG)
    for eG, eG in ipairs(S) do
        pcall(function()
            eG:Disconnect()
        end)
    end
    A.antiRagdollConns = {}
    S = A.antiRagdollConns
    local eG = aG:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
    local xG = aG:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 10)
    if not eG or not xG then
        return
    end
    local QG = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
    local VG = eG.StateChanged:Connect(function()
        local iG = eG:GetState()
        if
            iG == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
            or iG == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
            or iG == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
            or iG == Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp
        then
            local iG = false
            pcall(function()
                local OG = i.TpSettings.Tool or "Flying Carpet"
                local UG = aG:FindFirstChild(OG)
                if UG and xG then
                    for OG, OG in ipairs(xG:GetChildren()) do
                        if OG:IsA("BodyVelocity") or OG:IsA("BodyPosition") or OG:IsA("BodyGyro") then
                            iG = true
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            if not iG then
                eG:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
            end
            dG(aG)
            pcall(function()
                a.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = eG
            end)
            pcall(function()
                local iG = C:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts")
                if iG then
                    iG = iG:FindFirstChild("PlayerModule")
                end
                if iG then
                    require(iG):GetControls():Enable()
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
    table.insert(S, VG)
    local VG = aG.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(iG)
        if fG(iG) then
            nG(aG)
        end
    end)
    table.insert(S, VG)
    pcall(function()
        local nG = n:FindFirstChild("Packages")
        if nG then
            local fG = nG:FindFirstChild("Net")
            if fG then
                local nG = fG:FindFirstChild("RE/CombatService/ApplyImpulse")
                if nG and nG:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                    local fG = nG.OnClientEvent:Connect(function()
                        local nG = eG:GetState()
                        if
                            nG == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
                            or nG == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
                            or nG == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
                            or nG == Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp
                        then
                            pcall(function()
                                xG.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                            end)
                        end
                    end)
                    table.insert(S, fG)
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    local nG = R.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local fG = eG:GetState()
        if
            fG == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
            or fG == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
            or fG == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
            or fG == Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp
        then
            dG(aG)
            local fG = xG.Velocity
            if (fG - QG).Magnitude > 40 and fG.Magnitude > 25 then
                xG.Velocity = fG.Unit * math.min(fG.Magnitude, 15)
            end
        end
        QG = xG.Velocity
    end)
    table.insert(S, nG)
    dG(aG)
end
local function dG()
    CG = false
    for CG, CG in ipairs(S) do
        pcall(function()
            CG:Disconnect()
        end)
    end
    A.antiRagdollConns = {}
    S = A.antiRagdollConns
end
local function A(S)
    rG()
    dG()
    if not S then
        return
    end
    local S = C.Character
    if S then
        task.spawn(function()
            JG(S)
        end)
    end
    C.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(S)
        task.spawn(function()
            JG(S)
        end)
    end)
end
if k > 0 then
    RG(k)
end
if i.AntiRagdollV2 then
    A(true)
end
local k = {}
local S = false
local CG = nil
local rG = nil
local dG = nil
local function nG()
    S = false
    for fG, fG in ipairs(k) do
        if typeof(fG) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
            fG:Disconnect()
        end
    end
    k = {}
end
local function fG()
    if S then
        return
    end
    S = true
    local JG = C.Character
    if not JG then
        S = false
        return
    end
    local aG = JG:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not aG then
        for JG, JG in ipairs(workspace.CurrentCamera:GetChildren()) do
            if JG.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" then
                aG = JG
                break
            end
        end
    end
    if not aG then
        S = false
        return
    end
    local JG = aG.CFrame - aG.CFrame.Position
    CG = JG
    rG = aG.Position
    dG = aG
    local rG = R.Stepped:Connect(function()
        if not S then
            return
        end
        for eG, eG in ipairs(r:GetPlayers()) do
            if eG ~= C and eG.Character then
                for xG, xG in ipairs(eG.Character:GetChildren()) do
                    if xG:IsA("BasePart") then
                        xG.CanCollide = false
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    table.insert(k, rG)
    local rG = R.Stepped:Connect(function()
        if not S or not aG or not aG.Parent then
            return
        end
        aG.CFrame = CFrame.new(aG.Position) * JG
        aG.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
    end)
    table.insert(k, rG)
    local rG = coroutine.create(function()
        if _G.invisibleStealEnabled then
            aG.CFrame = aG.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 3, 0)
        end
        while S do
            R.Heartbeat:Wait()
            if not aG or not aG.Parent then
                break
            end
            local JG = aG.Velocity
            aG.Velocity = JG * 10000 + Vector3.new(0, 10000, 0)
            R.RenderStepped:Wait()
            if aG and aG.Parent then
                aG.Velocity = JG
            end
            R.Stepped:Wait()
            if aG and aG.Parent then
                aG.Velocity = JG + Vector3.new(0, 0.1, 0)
            end
        end
    end)
    coroutine.resume(rG)
    table.insert(k, rG)
end
local function k()
    if S then
        return
    end
    fG()
    task.delay(0.4, function()
        nG()
        task.wait(0.05)
        if dG and dG.Parent and CG then
            pcall(function()
                dG.CFrame = CFrame.new(dG.Position) * CG
                dG.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end)
        end
    end)
end
_G.dropBrainrot = k
do
    local S = nil
    local CG = nil
    local rG = nil
    local function dG()
        local nG = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        if not nG then
            return nil
        end
        for fG, fG in ipairs(nG:GetChildren()) do
            local nG = fG:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
            if nG then
                local JG = nG:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SurfaceGui", true)
                if JG then
                    local nG = JG:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel", true)
                    if nG then
                        local JG = nG.Text:lower()
                        if JG:find(C.DisplayName:lower(), 1, true) or JG:find(C.Name:lower(), 1, true) then
                            return fG
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return nil
    end
    local function nG()
        if not i.LineToBase then
            return
        end
        local fG = dG()
        if not fG or not fG.Parent then
            return
        end
        local dG = C.Character
        if not dG or not dG.Parent then
            return
        end
        local JG = dG:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not JG or not JG.Parent then
            return
        end
        if S then
            pcall(function()
                S:Destroy()
            end)
        end
        if CG then
            pcall(function()
                CG:Destroy()
            end)
        end
        CG = JG:FindFirstChild("PlotBeamAttach_Player") or Instance.new("Attachment")
        CG.Name = "PlotBeamAttach_Player"
        CG.Position = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        CG.Parent = JG
        local dG = fG:FindFirstChild("MainRootPart") or fG:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
        if not dG or not dG.Parent then
            return
        end
        rG = dG:FindFirstChild("PlotBeamAttach_Plot") or Instance.new("Attachment")
        rG.Name = "PlotBeamAttach_Plot"
        rG.Position = Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
        rG.Parent = dG
        S = JG:FindFirstChild("PlotBeam") or Instance.new("Beam")
        S.Name = "PlotBeam"
        S.Attachment0 = CG
        S.Attachment1 = rG
        S.FaceCamera = true
        S.LightEmission = 0.5
        S.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255))
        S.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0)
        S.Width0 = 0.7
        S.Width1 = 0.7
        S.TextureMode = Enum.TextureMode.Wrap
        S.TextureSpeed = 0
        S.Parent = JG
    end
    local function dG()
        if S then
            pcall(function()
                S:Destroy()
            end)
        end
        if CG then
            pcall(function()
                CG:Destroy()
            end)
        end
        if rG then
            pcall(function()
                rG:Destroy()
            end)
        end
        S = nil
        CG = nil
        rG = nil
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        local rG = 0
        R.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not i.LineToBase then
                return
            end
            rG = rG + 1
            if rG >= 30 then
                rG = 0
                if not S or not S.Parent or not CG or not CG.Parent then
                    pcall(nG)
                end
            end
        end)
    end)
    C.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(S)
        task.wait(0.5)
        if i.LineToBase and S then
            pcall(nG)
        end
    end)
    if C.Character then
        task.spawn(function()
            task.wait(0.2)
            if i.LineToBase then
                nG()
            end
        end)
    end
    _G.createPlotBeam = nG
    _G.resetPlotBeam = dG
end
task.spawn(function()
    local S = n:WaitForChild("Packages")
    local CG = n:WaitForChild("Datas")
    local rG = n:WaitForChild("Shared")
    local rG = n:WaitForChild("Utils")
    local rG = require(S:WaitForChild("Synchronizer"))
    local function S(dG)
        if not dG then
            return false
        end
        if typeof(dG) == "Instance" then
            return dG == C
        end
        if type(dG) == "string" then
            return dG == C.Name
        end
        return false
    end
    local function dG(nG)
        local fG, JG = pcall(function()
            local aG = getthreadidentity and getthreadidentity() or nil
            if setthreadidentity then
                setthreadidentity(8)
            end
            local eG = rG:GetTableFromChannel(nG)
            if aG and setthreadidentity then
                pcall(setthreadidentity, aG)
            end
            return eG
        end)
        if fG and type(JG) == "table" then
            return JG
        end
        return nil
    end
    local rG = require(CG:WaitForChild("Animals"))
    local nG = require(CG:WaitForChild("Mutations"))
    local fG = require(CG:WaitForChild("Traits"))
    local CG = {
        "",
        "K",
        "M",
        "B",
        "T",
        "Qa",
        "Qi",
        "Sx",
        "Sp",
        "Oc",
        "No",
        "Dc",
        "Ud",
        "Dd",
        "Td",
        "Qad",
        "Qid",
        "Sxd",
        "Spd",
        "Ocd",
        "Nod",
        "Vg",
        "Uvg",
        "Dvg",
        "Tvg",
    }
    local function JG(aG, eG)
        eG = eG or 1
        local xG = math.abs(aG)
        local QG = math.max(1, xG)
        local xG = math.floor(math.log(QG, 1000))
        local QG = CG[xG + 1] or "e+" .. xG
        local CG = aG * (10 ^ eG / 1000 ^ xG)
        local aG = math.floor(CG) / 10 ^ eG
        return (("%." .. eG .. "f"):format(aG)):gsub("%.?0+$", "") .. QG
    end
    local function CG(aG, eG, xG)
        local QG = rG[aG]
        if not QG then
            return 0
        end
        local aG = QG.Generation or QG.Price * 0.1
        local QG = 1
        if eG and eG ~= "None" then
            local VG = nG[eG]
            if VG and VG.Modifier then
                QG = QG + VG.Modifier
            end
        end
        local nG = false
        if type(xG) == "table" then
            for eG, eG in ipairs(xG) do
                if eG == "Sleepy" then
                    nG = true
                else
                    local xG = fG[eG]
                    if xG and xG.MultiplierModifier then
                        QG = QG + xG.MultiplierModifier
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        local fG = math.round(aG * QG)
        if nG then
            fG = math.round(fG * 0.5)
        end
        return fG
    end
    local nG = {}
    local fG = false
    local aG = nil
    local eG = {
        DeliveryHitbox = true,
        HumanoidRootPart = true,
        AnimalTarget = true,
        Spawn = true,
        Multiplier = true,
        MainRoot = true,
        StealHitbox = true,
    }
    local function xG(QG)
        if not QG:IsA("BasePart") then
            return false
        end
        if eG[QG.Name] then
            return false
        end
        local eG = QG.Name:lower()
        local VG = QG.Parent and QG.Parent.Name:lower() or ""
        return eG:find("base") or eG:find("claim") or VG:find("base") or VG:find("claim")
    end
    local function eG(QG)
        if not fG or not xG(QG) then
            return
        end
        if nG[QG] == nil then
            nG[QG] = QG.LocalTransparencyModifier
        end
        QG.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0.7
    end
    local xG
    xG = hookmetamethod(
        game,
        "__index",
        newcclosure(function(QG, VG)
            if not checkcaller() and VG == "LocalTransparencyModifier" and nG[QG] ~= nil then
                return nG[QG]
            end
            return xG(QG, VG)
        end)
    )
    local xG
    xG = hookmetamethod(
        game,
        "__newindex",
        newcclosure(function(QG, VG, iG)
            if not checkcaller() and VG == "LocalTransparencyModifier" and nG[QG] ~= nil then
                return
            end
            return xG(QG, VG, iG)
        end)
    )
    local function xG()
        fG = true
        if aG then
            aG:Disconnect()
        end
        local QG = a:GetDescendants()
        for VG = 1, #QG do
            eG(QG[VG])
        end
        aG = a.DescendantAdded:Connect(eG)
    end
    local function QG()
        fG = false
        if aG then
            aG:Disconnect()
            aG = nil
        end
        for aG, VG in pairs(nG) do
            pcall(function()
                aG.LocalTransparencyModifier = VG
            end)
        end
        nG = {}
    end
    _G._xrayEnable = xG
    _G._xrayDisable = QG
    C.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        task.wait(0.5)
        if not fG then
            return
        end
        local nG = a:GetDescendants()
        for fG = 1, #nG do
            eG(nG[fG])
        end
    end)
    if i.XrayBase then
        task.spawn(function()
            if not game:IsLoaded() then
                game.Loaded:Wait()
            end
            task.wait(2)
            if i.XrayBase then
                xG()
            end
        end)
    end
    local nG = true
    if i.DefaultToPriority and i.DefaultToHighest then
        i.DefaultToHighest = false
    end
    if i.DefaultToPriority and i.DefaultToNearest then
        i.DefaultToNearest = false
    end
    if i.DefaultToHighest and i.DefaultToNearest then
        i.DefaultToNearest = false
    end
    if not i.DefaultToPriority and not i.DefaultToHighest and not i.DefaultToNearest then
        i.DefaultToHighest = true
    end
    local fG = false
    local aG = false
    local eG = false
    if i.DefaultToNearest then
        fG = true
        i.StealNearest = true
        i.StealHighest = false
        i.StealPriority = false
        i.AutoTPPriority = true
    elseif i.DefaultToHighest then
        aG = true
        i.StealHighest = true
        i.StealNearest = false
        i.StealPriority = false
        i.AutoTPPriority = false
    elseif i.DefaultToPriority then
        eG = true
        i.StealPriority = true
        i.StealNearest = false
        i.StealHighest = false
        i.AutoTPPriority = true
    else
        fG = i.StealNearest
        aG = i.StealHighest
        eG = i.StealPriority
        if i.InstantSteal == nil then
            i.InstantSteal = false
        end
        if i.StealPriority then
            i.AutoTPPriority = true
        elseif i.StealNearest then
            i.AutoTPPriority = true
        elseif i.StealHighest then
            i.AutoTPPriority = false
        end
    end
    local xG = i.InstantSteal == true
    _G.NEAREST_INSTANT_MODE = (i.StealNearest == true and i.InstantSteal == true)
    local QG = false
    local VG = false
    local iG = 1
    local OG = nil
    local UG = nil
    local yG = {}
    local function KG()
        nG = fG == true or aG == true or eG == true
    end
    KG()
    local qG = {}
    local cG = {}
    local zG = nil
    local BG = nil
    local oG = {}
    local function YG(TG)
        if not TG or not TG.plot then
            return false
        end
        local IG = a:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        if not IG then
            return false
        end
        local pG = IG:FindFirstChild(TG.plot)
        if not pG then
            return false
        end
        local TG = dG(pG.Name)
        if TG then
            return S(TG.Owner)
        end
        return false
    end
    local function TG(IG)
        if not IG or IG == "None" then
            return ""
        end
        local pG = ""
        if IG == "Cursed" then
            pG = "<font color='rgb(200,0,0)'>Cur</font><font color='rgb(0,0,0)'>sed</font>"
        elseif IG == "Gold" then
            pG = "<font color='rgb(255,215,0)'>Gold</font>"
        elseif IG == "Diamond" then
            pG = "<font color='rgb(0,255,255)'>Diamond</font>"
        elseif IG == "YinYang" then
            pG = "<font color='rgb(255,255,255)'>Yin</font><font color='rgb(0,0,0)'>Yang</font>"
        elseif IG == "Candy" then
            pG = "<font color='rgb(255,105,180)'>Candy</font>"
        elseif IG == "Divine" then
            pG = "<font color='rgb(255,255,255)'>Divine</font>"
        elseif IG == "Rainbow" then
            local jG = {
                "rgb(255,0,0)",
                "rgb(255,127,0)",
                "rgb(255,255,0)",
                "rgb(0,255,0)",
                "rgb(0,0,255)",
                "rgb(75,0,130)",
                "rgb(148,0,211)",
            }
            for lG = 1, #IG do
                pG = pG .. "<font color='" .. jG[(lG - 1) % #jG + 1] .. "'>" .. IG:sub(lG, lG) .. "</font>"
            end
        elseif IG == "Radioactive" then
            pG = "<font color='rgb(132,255,0)'>Radioactive</font>"
        elseif IG == "Galaxy" then
            pG = "<font color='rgb(170,85,255)'>Galaxy</font>"
        else
            pG = IG
        end
        return "<font weight='800'>" .. pG .. " </font>"
    end
    local function IG(pG)
        if pG == "Gold" then
            return Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 0)
        elseif pG == "Diamond" then
            return Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 255)
        elseif pG == "Cursed" then
            return Color3.fromRGB(200, 0, 0)
        elseif pG == "YinYang" then
            return Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        elseif pG == "Candy" then
            return Color3.fromRGB(255, 105, 180)
        elseif pG == "Divine" then
            return Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        elseif pG == "Rainbow" then
            return Color3.fromRGB(148, 0, 211)
        elseif pG == "Radioactive" then
            return Color3.fromRGB(132, 255, 0)
        elseif pG == "Galaxy" then
            return Color3.fromRGB(170, 85, 255)
        end
        return Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 120)
    end
    local function pG(jG)
        local lG = jG and jG.petName or "Unknown"
        return lG
    end
    local function jG(lG)
        local WG = lG and lG.mutation
        if WG and WG ~= "None" and WG ~= "" then
            return TG(WG), true
        end
        return "", false
    end
    local function TG()
        local lG = {}
        for WG, WG in ipairs(yG) do
            if WG.genValue >= 1 and not YG(WG) then
                table.insert(
                    lG,
                    {
                        petName = WG.name,
                        mpsText = WG.genText,
                        mpsValue = WG.genValue,
                        owner = WG.owner,
                        plot = WG.plot,

                    JB.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
                    JB.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
                    JB.ZIndex = 4
                    local aB = Instance.new("TextLabel", rB)
                    aB.Size = UDim2.new(1, -108.0, 0, 16)
                    aB.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(38, 18)
                    aB.BackgroundTransparency = 1

                    aB.RichText = QB

                    aB.TextSize = 12
                    aB.TextColor3 = IG(CB.mutation)
                    aB.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                    aB.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None
                    aB.ClipsDescendants = false
                    aB.ZIndex = 4
                    local QB = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint", aB)
                    QB.MinTextSize = 8
                    QB.MaxTextSize = 12
                    aB.Visible = xB ~= ""
                    oG[PB] = {
                        button = rB,
                        selectedOverlay = RB,
                        rankBox = dB,
                        rankBoxStroke = nB,
                        rank = fB,
                        info = eB,
                        rate = JB,
                        mutation = aB,
                        petData = CB,
                    }
                    rB.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                        if UG == CB.uid then
                            UG = nil
                            OG = nil
                            _G.NEAREST_INSTANT_MODE = (fG and xG)
                        else
                            iG = PB
                            OG = CB.uid
                            UG = CB.uid
                            nG = true
                            _G.NEAREST_INSTANT_MODE = false
                        end
                        P.ListNeedsRedraw = true
                        if brainrotESPEnabled then
                            task.defer(function()
                                pcall(function()
                                    _espLastHash = nil
                                    refreshBrainrotESP()
                                end)
                            end)
                        end
                        sG(nG, TG())
                    end)
                end
            end
            P.ListNeedsRedraw = false
            local PB = #oG
            local CB = 0
            if PB > 0 then
                CB = PB * 38 + (PB - 1) * 5
            end
            XG.Size = UDim2.new(1, -0.0, 0, CB)
            WG.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, CB + 0)
        end
        for PB, PB in ipairs(oG) do
            local oG = PB.petData and UG and PB.petData.uid == UG
            PB.button.ZIndex = 1
            PB.button.BackgroundTransparency = 0
            PB.button.BackgroundColor3 = oG and Color3.fromRGB(44, 128, 79) or GG.SURF2
            if PB.selectedOverlay then
                PB.selectedOverlay.Visible = oG
                PB.selectedOverlay.ZIndex = 2
                PB.selectedOverlay.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 10, 12)
                PB.selectedOverlay.BackgroundTransparency = oG and 0.9 or 1
            end
            if PB.rankBox then
                PB.rankBox.BackgroundColor3 = oG and Color3.fromRGB(33, 97, 60) or GG.SURF
                PB.rankBox.ZIndex = 3
            end
            if PB.rankBoxStroke then
                PB.rankBoxStroke.Color = oG and Color3.fromRGB(33, 97, 60) or GG.AQUA_STROKE
                PB.rankBoxStroke.Thickness = 1
                PB.rankBoxStroke.Transparency = oG and 1 or 0.45
            end
            if PB.rank then
                PB.rank.ZIndex = 4
                PB.rank.TextColor3 = oG and Color3.fromRGB(240, 255, 240) or GG.TEXT
            end
            if PB.info then
                PB.info.ZIndex = 4
                PB.info.RichText = false
                if PB.petData then
                    PB.info.Text = pG(PB.petData)
                end
                PB.info.TextColor3 = oG and Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0) or GG.TEXT
            end
            if PB.mutation then
                PB.mutation.ZIndex = 4
                if PB.petData then
                    local pG, GG = jG(PB.petData)
                    PB.mutation.RichText = GG
                    PB.mutation.Text = pG
                    PB.mutation.Visible = pG ~= ""
                end
                PB.mutation.TextColor3 = oG and Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                    or IG(PB.petData and PB.petData.mutation)
            end
            if PB.rate then
                PB.rate.ZIndex = 4
                if PB.petData and PB.petData.mpsText then
                    PB.rate.Text = PB.petData.mpsText
                end
                PB.rate.TextColor3 = oG and Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0) or Color3.fromRGB(56, 214, 110)
            end
        end
        local oG = NG and NG[iG]
        P.SelectedPetData = oG
        if SG then
            if not fG then
                if oG then
                    MG.Text = string.format("%s - %s", oG.petName or "Unknown", oG.mpsText or "")
                else
                    MG.Text = "Searching..."
                end
            end
        else
            MG.Text = "Disabled"
            if zG then
                zG:Cancel()
                zG = nil
            end
            bG.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
        end
        FG.Text = string.format("%d%%", math.clamp(math.floor(bG.Size.X.Scale * 100 + 0.5), 0, 100))
        XG.Size = UDim2.new(1, -0.0, 0, math.max(0, vG.AbsoluteContentSize.Y))
        WG.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, math.max(0, vG.AbsoluteContentSize.Y) + 0)
    end
    vG:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(function()
        XG.Size = UDim2.new(1, -0.0, 0, math.max(0, vG.AbsoluteContentSize.Y))
        WG.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, math.max(0, vG.AbsoluteContentSize.Y) + 0)
    end)
    P.UpdateAutoStealUI = function()
        sG(nG, TG())
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        while bG and bG.Parent do
            local oG = math.clamp(math.floor(bG.Size.X.Scale * 100 + 0.5), 0, 100)
            FG.Text = tostring(oG) .. "%"
            task.wait(0.05)
        end
    end)

    end)
    local KG = Instance.new("TextButton", lG)
    KG.Name = "CustomizePriorityButton"
    KG.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, math.floor(38 * AG))
    KG.BackgroundColor3 = b.AQUA2
    KG.BackgroundTransparency = 0.02
    KG.Text = "CUSTOMIZE PRIORITY"
    KG.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    KG.TextSize = 12 * AG
    KG.TextColor3 = b.TEXT

    tG(KG, 11)
    DG(KG, b.AQUA_STROKE, 1, 0.2)
    mG(KG, b.AQUA, b.AQUA2, 0)
    KG.Visible = false
    KG.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        EG(KG, 0.14, { BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(188, 60, 60) })

        EG(KG, 0.14, { BackgroundColor3 = b.AQUA2 })
    end)
    KG.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        settingsGui.Enabled = true
        _G.SetHazeUIPage("Priority")
    end)
    LG = HG(lG, "Instant Steal", 180 * AG)
    LG.button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        xG = not xG
        if xG then
            QG = false
            VG = false
        else

        end

        _G.NEAREST_INSTANT_MODE = (fG and xG)

    task.spawn(function()
        while true do

            if not xG then
                continue
            end
            xG = false
            QG = false
