            i.InstantSteal = false
            _G.NEAREST_INSTANT_MODE = false
            task.wait(0.05)
            xG = true
            i.InstantSteal = true
            _G.NEAREST_INSTANT_MODE = (fG and true)
        end
    end)
    local function b(KG)
        if not KG then
            return nil
        end
        local oG = cG[KG.uid]
        if oG and oG.Parent then
            return oG
        end
        local oG = a.Plots:FindFirstChild(KG.plot)
        if not oG then
            return nil
        end
        local IG = oG:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
        if not IG then
            return nil
        end
        local pG = dG(oG.Name)
        local jG = pG and pG.AnimalList
        if not jG then
            local pG = IG:FindFirstChild(KG.slot)
            if pG then
                local lG = pG:FindFirstChild("Base")
                local pG = lG and lG:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
                if pG then
                    local lG = pG:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")
                    if lG then
                        for pG, pG in ipairs(lG:GetChildren()) do
                            if pG:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
                                cG[KG.uid] = pG
                                return pG
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            return nil
        end
        if not jG then
            return nil
        end
        local pG = KG.name and KG.name:lower() or ""
        local lG = KG.slot
        local WG = nil
        for XG, tG in pairs(jG) do
            if type(tG) == "table" and tostring(XG) == lG then
                local jG, lG = tG.Index, rG[tG.Index]
                if lG and (lG.DisplayName or jG):lower() == pG then
                    WG = IG:FindFirstChild(tostring(XG))
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        if not WG then
            WG = IG:FindFirstChild(KG.slot)
        end
        if WG then
            local IG = WG:FindFirstChild("Base")
            local jG = IG and IG:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
            if jG then
                local IG = jG:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")
                if IG then
                    for lG, lG in ipairs(IG:GetChildren()) do
                        if lG:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and lG.Enabled and lG.ActionText == "Steal" then
                            cG[KG.uid] = lG
                            return lG
                        end
                    end
                end
                local IG = jG.Position
                local jG, lG = IG.X, IG.Z
                local WG = nil
                local XG = math.huge
                for tG, tG in pairs(oG:GetDescendants()) do
                    if tG:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and tG.Enabled and tG.ActionText == "Steal" then
                        local oG = tG.Parent
                        local GG = nil
                        if oG and oG:IsA("BasePart") then
                            GG = oG.Position
                        elseif oG and oG:IsA("Attachment") and oG.Parent and oG.Parent:IsA("BasePart") then
                            GG = oG.Parent.Position
                        end
                        if GG then
                            local oG = IG.Y
                            if pG:find("la secret combinasion") then
                                oG = IG.Y - 5
                            end
                            local IG = math.sqrt((GG.X - jG) ^ 2 + (GG.Z - lG) ^ 2)
                            if IG < 5 and GG.Y > oG then
                                local IG = GG.Y - oG
                                if IG < XG then
                                    XG = IG
                                    WG = tG
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                if WG then
                    cG[KG.uid] = WG
                    return WG
                end
            end
        end
        return nil
    end
    local function KG(oG)
        if qG[oG] then
            return
        end
        local IG = { holdCallbacks = {}, triggerCallbacks = {}, holdEndCallbacks = {}, ready = true }
        local pG, jG = pcall(getconnections, oG.PromptButtonHoldBegan)
        if pG and type(jG) == "table" then
            for pG, pG in ipairs(jG) do
                if type(pG.Function) == "function" then
                    table.insert(IG.holdCallbacks, pG.Function)
                end
            end
        end
        local pG, jG = pcall(getconnections, oG.Triggered)
        if pG and type(jG) == "table" then
            for pG, pG in ipairs(jG) do
                if type(pG.Function) == "function" then
                    table.insert(IG.triggerCallbacks, pG.Function)
                end
            end
        end
        local pG, jG = pcall(getconnections, oG.PromptButtonHoldEnded)
        if pG and type(jG) == "table" then
            for pG, pG in ipairs(jG) do
                if type(pG.Function) == "function" then
                    table.insert(IG.holdEndCallbacks, pG.Function)
                end
            end
        end
        if #IG.holdCallbacks > 0 or #IG.triggerCallbacks > 0 or #IG.holdEndCallbacks > 0 then
            qG[oG] = IG
        end
    end
    local function oG(IG)
        for pG, pG in ipairs(IG) do
            task.spawn(pG)
        end
    end
    local function IG(pG)
        local jG = dG(pG)
        if jG then
            return S(jG.Owner)
        end
        return false
    end
    local function pG()
        local jG = C.Character and C.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not jG then
            return nil, math.huge, nil
        end
        local lG = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        if not lG then
            return nil, math.huge, nil
        end
        local WG, XG, tG = nil, math.huge, nil
        for GG, GG in ipairs(lG:GetChildren()) do
            if IG(GG.Name) then
                continue
            end
            local IG = math.huge
            pcall(function()
                IG = (GG:GetPivot().Position - jG.Position).Magnitude
            end)
            if IG > 100 then
                continue
            end
            local IG = GG:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
            if not IG then
                continue
            end
            for lG, lG in ipairs(IG:GetChildren()) do
                local IG = lG:FindFirstChild("Base")
                local GG = IG and IG:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
                if not GG then
                    continue
                end
                local IG = (GG.Position - jG.Position).Magnitude
                if IG > 60 or IG >= XG then
                    continue
                end
                local jG = GG:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")
                if not jG then
                    continue
                end
                local GG = jG:FindFirstChildOfClass("ProximityPrompt")
                if GG and GG.Parent and GG.Enabled then
                    WG = GG
                    XG = IG
                    tG = lG.Name
                end
            end
        end
        return WG, XG, tG
    end
    local function IG(jG, lG)
        local WG = qG[jG]
        if not WG or not WG.ready then
            return false
        end
        WG.ready = false
        task.spawn(function()
            if BG ~= lG then
                if zG then
                    zG:Cancel()
                end
                wG.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
                BG = lG
            end
            if #WG.holdCallbacks > 0 then
                oG(WG.holdCallbacks)
            end
            wG.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
            wG.BackgroundTransparency = 0
            zG = f:Create(wG, TweenInfo.new(1.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0) })
            zG:Play()
            zG.Completed:Wait()
            if BG == lG and #WG.triggerCallbacks > 0 then
                oG(WG.triggerCallbacks)
            end
            WG.ready = true
        end)
        return true
    end
    local function oG(jG, lG)
        if not jG or not jG.Parent then
            return false
        end
        KG(jG)
        if not qG[jG] then
            return false
        end
        if BG ~= lG then
            if zG then
                zG:Cancel()
                zG = nil
            end
            wG.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
        end
        return IG(jG, lG)
    end
    local function qG()
        for BG, BG in pairs(cG) do
            if BG and BG.Parent then
                KG(BG)
            end
        end
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait(2) do
            if nG then
                qG()
            end
        end
    end)
    local KG = {}
    local function qG(BG)
        if not BG then
            return ""
        end
        local IG = ""
        for jG, lG in pairs(BG) do
            if type(lG) == "table" then
                IG = IG .. tostring(jG) .. tostring(lG.Index) .. tostring(lG.Mutation)
            end
        end
        return IG
    end
    local function BG(IG)
        local jG = false
        pcall(function()
            local lG = dG(IG.Name)
            if not lG then
                return
            end
            local WG = lG.AnimalList
            local XG = lG.Owner
            if
                not XG
                or S(XG)
                or typeof(XG) == "Instance" and not r:FindFirstChild(XG.Name)
                or type(XG) == "string" and not r:FindFirstChild(XG)
            then
                KG[IG.Name] = nil
                for S = #yG, 1, -1.0 do
                    if yG[S].plot == IG.Name then
                        table.remove(yG, S)
                        jG = true
                    end
                end
                return
            end
            if not WG then
                KG[IG.Name] = nil
                for S = #yG, 1, -1.0 do
                    if yG[S].plot == IG.Name then
                        table.remove(yG, S)
                        jG = true
                    end
                end
                return
            end
            local S = typeof(XG) == "Instance" and XG.Name or tostring(XG)
            local lG = qG(WG, S)
            if KG[IG.Name] == lG then
                return
            end
            for qG = #yG, 1, -1.0 do
                if yG[qG].plot == IG.Name then
                    table.remove(yG, qG)
                end
            end
            for qG, XG in pairs(WG) do
                if type(XG) == "table" then
                    local WG, tG = XG.Index, rG[XG.Index]
                    if tG then
                        local rG = XG.Mutation or "None"
                        if rG == "Yin Yang" then
                            rG = "YinYang"
                        end
                        local GG = XG.Traits and #XG.Traits > 0 and table.concat(XG.Traits, ", ") or "None"
                        local vG = CG(WG, XG.Mutation, XG.Traits)
                        local CG = "$" .. JG(vG) .. "/s"
                        table.insert(
                            yG,
                            {
                                name = tG.DisplayName or WG,
                                genText = CG,
                                genValue = vG,
                                mutation = rG,
                                traits = GG,
                                owner = S,
                                plot = IG.Name,
                                slot = tostring(qG),
                                uid = IG.Name .. "_" .. tostring(qG),
                            }
                        )
                    end
                end
            end
            KG[IG.Name] = lG
            jG = true
        end)
        if jG then
            table.sort(yG, function(S, CG)
                return S.genValue > CG.genValue
            end)
            P.AllAnimalsCache = yG
            P.ListNeedsRedraw = true
            if P.UpdateAutoStealUI then
                P.UpdateAutoStealUI()
            end
            if not hasShownPriorityAlert and i.AlertsEnabled then
                task.spawn(function()
                    local S = nil
                    for CG = 1, #X do
                        local rG = X[CG]
                        local CG = rG:lower()
                        for rG, rG in ipairs(yG) do
                            if rG.name and rG.name:lower() == CG then
                                S = rG
                                break
                            end
                        end
                        if S then
                            break
                        end
                    end
                    if S then
                        local CG = S.owner
                        local rG = nil
                        local JG = a:FindFirstChild("Plots") and a.Plots:FindFirstChild(S.plot)
                        if JG then
                            local qG = dG(JG.Name)
                            if qG and qG.Owner then
                                local JG = qG.Owner
                                if typeof(JG) == "Instance" then
                                    rG = JG
                                    CG = JG.Name
                                elseif type(JG) == "string" then
                                    CG = JG
                                    rG = r:FindFirstChild(JG)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        if not rG and CG then
                            rG = r:FindFirstChild(CG)
                        end
                        ShowPriorityAlert(S.name, S.genText, S.mutation, CG)
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    end
    local function S(CG)
        local rG, JG = nil, 0
        while not rG and JG < 40 do
            rG = dG(CG.Name)
            if not rG then
                JG = JG + 1
                task.wait(0.07)
            end
        end
        if not rG then
            return
        end
        BG(CG)
        local function rG(JG)
            if not JG then
                return
            end
            JG.ChildAdded:Connect(function()
                task.wait(0.15)
                BG(CG)
            end)
            JG.ChildRemoved:Connect(function()
                for JG = #yG, 1, -1.0 do
                    if yG[JG].plot == CG.Name then
                        table.remove(yG, JG)
                    end
                end
                KG[CG.Name] = nil
                _getPetsCache = nil
                for JG in pairs(cG) do
                    if JG:sub(1, #CG.Name) == CG.Name then
                        cG[JG] = nil
                    end
                end
                P.ListNeedsRedraw = true
                if P.UpdateAutoStealUI then
                    P.UpdateAutoStealUI()
                end
                task.wait(0.15)
                BG(CG)
            end)
        end
        local JG = CG:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
        rG(JG)
        CG.ChildAdded:Connect(function(JG)
            if JG.Name == "AnimalPodiums" then
                rG(JG)
                BG(CG)
            end
        end)
        CG.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(rG)
            if rG.Name == "AnimalPodiums" then
                for rG = #yG, 1, -1.0 do
                    if yG[rG].plot == CG.Name then
                        table.remove(yG, rG)
                    end
                end
                KG[CG.Name] = nil
                _getPetsCache = nil
                P.ListNeedsRedraw = true
                if P.UpdateAutoStealUI then
                    P.UpdateAutoStealUI()
                end
            end
        end)
        task.spawn(function()
            while CG.Parent do
                task.wait(10)
                BG(CG)
            end
        end)
    end
    local CG = a:WaitForChild("Plots", 8)
    if CG then
        for rG, rG in ipairs(CG:GetChildren()) do
            S(rG)
        end
        CG.ChildAdded:Connect(function(rG)
            task.wait(0.5)
            S(rG)
        end)
        CG.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(S)

                end
            end
            P.ListNeedsRedraw = true
            if P.UpdateAutoStealUI then
                P.UpdateAutoStealUI()
            end
        end)
    end

    local function S(CG)
        if #CG == 0 then
            return
        end
        if UG then
            for rG, dG in ipairs(CG) do
                if dG.uid == UG then
                    if iG ~= rG then
                        iG = rG
                        OG = dG.uid
                    end
                    MG.Text = string.format("%s - %s", dG.petName or "Unknown", dG.mpsText or "")
                    return
                end
            end
            UG = nil
        end
        if eG then
            for rG, rG in ipairs(X) do
                local dG = rG:lower()
                for rG, JG in ipairs(CG) do
                    if JG.petName and JG.petName:lower() == dG then
                        if iG ~= rG then
                            iG = rG
                            OG = JG.uid
                        end
                        return
                    end
                end
            end
            if iG ~= 1 then
                iG = 1
                OG = CG[1] and CG[1].uid
            end
        elseif fG then
            local rG = C.Character
            local dG = rG and rG:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if dG then
                local rG, JG = 1, math.huge
                for eG, KG in ipairs(CG) do
                    local qG = KG.animalData and M(KG.animalData)
                    if qG and qG:IsA("BasePart") then
                        local KG = (dG.Position - qG.Position).Magnitude
                        if KG < JG then
                            JG = KG
                            rG = eG
                        end
                    end
                end
                if CG[rG] then
                    local dG = CG[rG]
                    MG.Text = string.format("%s - %s", dG.petName or "Unknown", dG.mpsText or "")
                end
                if not xG and iG ~= rG then
                    iG = rG
                    OG = CG[rG] and CG[rG].uid
                end
            end
        elseif aG then
            if iG ~= 1 then
                iG = 1
                OG = CG[1] and CG[1].uid
            end
        end
    end
    R.Heartbeat:Connect(function()

    local b = nil
    local S = nil
    local CG = nil
    local function rG()
        if not nG or not i.TracerEnabled or i.StealNearest then
            if b then
                b:Destroy()
                b = nil
            end
            if S then
                S:Destroy()
                S = nil
            end
            if CG then
                CG:Destroy()
                CG = nil
            end
            return
        end
        local dG = TG()
        if #dG == 0 then
            if b then
                b.Enabled = false
            end
            return
        end
        if iG > #dG then
            iG = #dG
        end
        if iG < 1 then
            iG = 1
        end
        local nG = dG[iG] or dG[1]
        local dG = M(nG.animalData)
        local fG = C.Character
        local JG = fG and fG:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if JG and dG then
            if not S or S.Parent ~= JG then
                if S then
                    S:Destroy()
                end
                S = Instance.new("Attachment", JG)
            end
            if not CG or CG.Parent ~= dG then
                if CG then
                    CG:Destroy()
                end
                CG = Instance.new("Attachment", dG)
            end
            if not b then
                b = Instance.new("Beam", JG)
                b.FaceCamera = true
                b.Width0 = 0.8
                b.Width1 = 0.8
                b.TextureMode = Enum.TextureMode.Static
                b.TextureSpeed = 3
            end
            b.Attachment0 = S
            b.Attachment1 = CG
            b.Enabled = true
            local S = {
                Cursed = Color3.fromRGB(200, 0, 0),
                Gold = Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 0),
                Diamond = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 255),
                YinYang = Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 220),
                Rainbow = Color3.fromRGB(255, 100, 200),
                Lava = Color3.fromRGB(255, 100, 20),
                Candy = Color3.fromRGB(255, 105, 180),
                Divine = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
            }
            local CG = nG and nG.mutation and S[nG.mutation] or W.Accent1
            b.Color = ColorSequence.new(CG)
        else
            if b then
                b.Enabled = false
            end
        end
    end
    R.Heartbeat:Connect(rG)
end)

local S = m.floatActive
R.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    if not m.floatActive then
        return
    end
    local S = os.clock()
    if S - (_lastFloatTick or 0) < 0.05 then
        return
    end
    _lastFloatTick = S
    if b.bodyController and C.Character then
        local S = C.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if S then
            b.bodyController.Position = Vector3.new(S.Position.X, b.bodyController.Position.Y, S.Position.Z)
        end
    end
end)
local b = m.isTpMoving
_G._isTargetPlotUnlocked = function(S)
    local CG, rG = pcall(function()
        local dG = a:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        if not dG then
            return false
        end
        local nG = dG:FindFirstChild(S)
        if not nG then
            return false
        end
        local S = nG:FindFirstChild("Unlock")
        if not S then
            return true
        end
        local dG = {}
        for nG, nG in pairs(S:GetChildren()) do
            local S = nil
            if nG:IsA("Model") then
                pcall(function()
                    S = nG:GetPivot().Position
                end)
            elseif nG:IsA("BasePart") then
                S = nG.Position
            end
            if S then
                table.insert(dG, { Object = nG, Height = S.Y })
            end
        end
        table.sort(dG, function(S, nG)
            return S.Height < nG.Height
        end)
        if #dG == 0 then
            return true
        end
        local S = dG[1].Object
        for dG, dG in ipairs(S:GetDescendants()) do
            if dG:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and dG.Enabled then
                return false
            end
        end
        for dG, dG in ipairs(S:GetChildren()) do
            if dG:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and dG.Enabled then
                return false
            end
        end
        return true
    end)
    return CG and rG or false
end
local function S()
    if m.isTpMoving then
        return
    end
    if m.carpetSpeedEnabled then
        g(false)
        if u then
            u.Text = "OFF"
            u.TextColor3 = W.Error
        end
    end
    local CG = nil
    if i.AutoTPPriority then
        local rG = nil
        local dG = P.AllAnimalsCache
        if dG and type(dG) == "table" then
            for nG, nG in ipairs(X) do
                local fG = nG:lower()
                for nG, nG in ipairs(dG) do
                    if nG and nG.name and nG.name:lower() == fG and nG.owner ~= C.Name then
                        rG = nG
                        break
                    end
                end
                if rG then
                    break
                end
            end
            if not rG then
                for nG, nG in ipairs(dG) do
                    if nG and nG.owner ~= C.Name then
                        rG = nG
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if rG then
            CG = rG
        else
            if not P.SelectedPetData then
                F("ERROR", "No target selected!")
                return
            end
            CG = P.SelectedPetData.animalData
        end
    else
        if not P.SelectedPetData then
            F("ERROR", "No target selected!")
            return
        end
        CG = P.SelectedPetData.animalData
    end
    if not CG then
        return
    end
    local rG = C.Character
    local dG = rG and rG:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local nG = rG and rG:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
    if not dG or not nG or nG.Health <= 0 then
        return
    end
    m.isTpMoving = true
    b = m.isTpMoving
    local fG, JG = pcall(function()
        local JG = M(CG)
        if not JG then
            error("targetPart nil")
        end
        local aG
        if JG:IsA("Attachment") then
            aG = JG.WorldCFrame
        elseif JG:IsA("BasePart") then
            aG = JG.CFrame
        else
            local eG, xG = pcall(function()
                return JG:GetPivot()
            end)
            if eG then
                aG = xG
            end
        end
        if not aG then
            error("targetCF nil")
        end
        local aG = JG.Position
        local eG = i.TpSettings.Tool
        local xG = C.Backpack:FindFirstChild(eG) or rG:FindFirstChild(eG)
        local eG = C.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Quantum Cloner") or rG:FindFirstChild("Quantum Cloner")
        I(nG)
        local rG = aG.Y > 10
        if i.TpSettings.TeleportV2 then
            local eG, xG = l(JG, nG, dG)
            if not eG then
                error("Teleport V2 failed: " .. tostring(xG))
            end
        else
            local l = Y(JG)
            if not l then
                error("signPart nil")
            end
            local Y = l.CFrame
            local eG = Y.RightVector
            local xG = -eG
            local eG = Y.LookVector
            local QG = -Y.LookVector
            local Y = G(dG.Position)
            if not Y then
                error("normal tp MED point nil")
            end
            dG.Velocity = Vector3.zero
            if nG and nG.Parent then
                nG:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
            end
            task.wait(0.01)
            if not dG or not dG.Parent then
                error("hrp lost during normal jump start")
            end
            local Y
            if aG.Y <= 6.313370704650879 then
                local G = l.Position + eG * 20
                local aG = l.Position + QG * 20
                local VG = dG.Position
                local iG = (Vector3.new(G.X, 0, G.Z) - Vector3.new(VG.X, 0, VG.Z)).Magnitude
                local OG = (Vector3.new(aG.X, 0, aG.Z) - Vector3.new(VG.X, 0, VG.Z)).Magnitude
                local VG = iG < OG and G or aG
                Y = Vector3.new(VG.X, -4.8, VG.Z)
            else
                local G = l.Position + QG * 15
                local aG = l.Position + eG * 15
                local eG = dG.Position
                local QG = Vector3.new(eG.X, 0, eG.Z)
                local eG = (Vector3.new(G.X, 0, G.Z) - QG).Magnitude
                local VG = (Vector3.new(aG.X, 0, aG.Z) - QG).Magnitude
                local QG = eG < VG and G or aG
                Y = Vector3.new(QG.X, l.Position.Y + 4, QG.Z)
            end
            local l = dG.Position
            local G = math.max(l.Y, 14)
            local aG = Vector3.new(Y.X, G, Y.Z)
            if l.Y < 19 then
                local G = Vector3.new(l.X, 19, l.Z)
                local aG = math.clamp(math.ceil((19 - l.Y) / 1.5), 8, 25)
                for eG = 1, aG do
                    if not dG or not dG.Parent then
                        break
                    end
                    local QG = eG / aG
                    local aG = l:Lerp(G, QG)
                    dG.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                    dG.CFrame = CFrame.new(aG)
                    task.wait(0.03)
                end
                l = dG.Position
            end
            local G = Vector3.new(Y.X, 19, Y.Z)
            local aG = (Vector3.new(l.X, 0, l.Z) - Vector3.new(Y.X, 0, Y.Z)).Magnitude
            local eG = math.clamp(math.ceil(aG / 2), 20, 100)
            local QG = aG > 400 and 0.025 or aG > 250 and 0.018 or aG > 150 and 0.013 or 0.01
            for aG = 1, eG do
                if not dG or not dG.Parent then
                    break
                end
                local VG = aG / eG
                local aG = l:Lerp(G, VG)
                dG.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                dG.CFrame = CFrame.new(aG)
                task.wait(QG)
            end
            local l = dG.Position
            local G = math.clamp(math.ceil(math.abs(l.Y - Y.Y) / 1.5), 8, 25)
            for aG = 1, G do
                if not dG or not dG.Parent then
                    break
                end
                local eG = aG / G
                local G = l:Lerp(Y, eG)
                dG.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                dG.CFrame = CFrame.new(G)
                task.wait(0.01)
            end
            dG.Velocity = Vector3.zero
            dG.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(Y, Y + xG)
            dG.Velocity = Vector3.zero
            o(function()
                return dG and dG.Parent and (dG.Position - Y).Magnitude <= 3
            end, 1.5)
            if rG or not _G._isTargetPlotUnlocked(CG.plot) then
                p(nG, dG)
                B(0.05)
                if rG then
                    dG.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                    B(0.01)
                    dG.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                    dG.CFrame = dG.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, -2.5)
                    dG.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                else
                    dG.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                    dG.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                    B(0.03)
                    z(0.15)
                end
                B(0.07)
                local z = dG.Position
                B(0.01)
                local Y = o(function()
                    return dG and dG.Parent and (dG.Position - z).Magnitude <= 2
                end, 0.75)
                if not Y then
                    error("moved away from mini TP position before clone")
                end
                dG.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                dG.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                task.wait(0.1)
                q()
                local Y = o(function()
                    return dG and dG.Parent and (dG.Position - z).Magnitude >= 0.5
                end, 5)
                while _G.isCloning do
                    task.wait()
                end
                task.wait(0.2)
            end
        end
        task.wait(0.25)
        I(nG)
        local z = JG.Position
        local Y = dG.Position
        local I = z.Y - Y.Y
        local p = z.Y > 25
        local l = nil
        if I > 2 and not p then
            l = Instance.new("Part")
            l.Name = "XiTempPlatform"
            l.Size = Vector3.new(6, 1, 6)
            l.Position = Vector3.new(z.X, z.Y - 2.5, z.Z)
            l.Color = Color3.new(1, 0, 0)
            l.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
            l.Anchored = true
            l.CanCollide = true
            l.Transparency = 0.3
            l.Parent = a
            task.spawn(function()
                local I = tick()
                while tick() - I < 25 do
                    if C:GetAttribute("Stealing") then
                        break
                    end
                    task.wait(0.1)
                end
                if l and l.Parent then
                    l:Destroy()
                end
            end)
        end
        local I = l and Vector3.new(z.X, z.Y - 2, z.Z) or z
        if p then
            local p = Vector3.new(Y.X, z.Y - 2, Y.Z)
            local l = math.clamp(math.ceil(math.abs(p.Y - Y.Y) / 1.5), 6, 30)
            for G = 1, l do
                if not dG or not dG.Parent then
                    break
                end
                if C:GetAttribute("Stealing") then
                    break
                end
                local CG = G / l
                dG.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                dG.CFrame = CFrame.new(Y:Lerp(p, CG))
                task.wait(0.02)
            end
            local p = dG.Position
            local l = Vector3.new(z.X, z.Y - 2, z.Z)
            local z = (Vector3.new(p.X, 0, p.Z) - Vector3.new(l.X, 0, l.Z)).Magnitude
            local G = math.clamp(math.ceil(z / 2), 10, 60)
            local CG = z > 200 and 0.02 or 0.015
            for z = 1, G do
                if not dG or not dG.Parent then
                    break
                end
                if C:GetAttribute("Stealing") then
                    break
                end
                local rG = z / G
                dG.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                dG.CFrame = CFrame.new(p:Lerp(l, rG))
                task.wait(CG)
            end
        else
            local z = Vector3.new(Y.X, I.Y, Y.Z)
            local p = math.abs(z.Y - Y.Y)
            local l = math.clamp(math.ceil(p / 1.5), 6, 20)
            for p = 1, l do
                if not dG or not dG.Parent then
                    break
                end
                if C:GetAttribute("Stealing") then
                    break
                end
                local G = p / l
                dG.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                dG.CFrame = CFrame.new(Y:Lerp(z, G))
                task.wait(0.01)
            end
            local z = dG.Position
            local Y = Vector3.new(I.X, I.Y, I.Z)
            local p = (Vector3.new(z.X, 0, z.Z) - Vector3.new(I.X, 0, I.Z)).Magnitude
            local l = math.clamp(math.ceil(p / 2), 15, 80)
            local G = p > 200 and 0.02 or 0.01
            for p = 1, l do
                if not dG or not dG.Parent then
                    break
                end
                if C:GetAttribute("Stealing") then
                    break
                end
                local CG = p / l
                dG.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                dG.CFrame = CFrame.new(z:Lerp(Y, CG))
                task.wait(G)
            end
            local z = dG.Position
            local Y = math.abs(z.Y - I.Y)
            local p = math.clamp(math.ceil(Y / 1.5), 5, 15)
            for Y = 1, p do
                if not dG or not dG.Parent then
                    break
                end
                if C:GetAttribute("Stealing") then
                    break
                end
                local l = Y / p
                dG.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                dG.CFrame = CFrame.new(z:Lerp(I, l))
                task.wait(0.01)
            end
        end
        dG.Velocity = Vector3.zero
    end)
    m.isTpMoving = false
    b = m.isTpMoving
    if not fG then
    end
end

local I = false
local function p()
    local function l(G)
        if not G then
            return nil
        end
        for b, b in ipairs(G:GetDescendants()) do
            if b:IsA("RemoteEvent") and b.Name:sub(1, 3) == "RE/" then
                return b
            end
        end
        return nil
    end
    return l(n) or l(game:GetService("CoreGui")) or l(workspace)
end

local function Y(I)
    task.spawn(l)
    if m.carpetSpeedEnabled then
        g(false)
        if u then
            u.Text = "OFF"
            u.TextColor3 = W.Error
        end
    end
    local p = C.Character
    if not p then
        pcall(function()
            C:LoadCharacter()
        end)
        if I then
            local l
            l = C.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
                if l then
                    l:Disconnect()
                end
                task.defer(function()
                    pcall(I)
                end)
            end)
        end
        return
    end
    pcall(function()
        local l = p:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local G = p:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not (G and l) then
            return
        end
        z(p)
        local b = type(i.TpSettings) == "table" and i.TpSettings.Tool
        local CG = b or V.TpSettings and V.TpSettings.Tool
        if CG then
            local b = C.Backpack:FindFirstChild(CG) or p:FindFirstChild(CG)
            if b then
                l:EquipTool(b)
            end
        end
        G.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 15000, 0)
        R.Heartbeat:Wait()
        z(p)
        R.Heartbeat:Wait()
        l = p:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        G = p:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not (l and G) then
            return
        end
        pcall(function()
            l.Health = 0
        end)
        pcall(function()
            l:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
        end)
        if l.Health > 0 then
            pcall(function()
                l:TakeDamage(l.MaxHealth * 99)
            end)
        end
        if l.Health > 0 then
            pcall(function()
                p:BreakJoints()
            end)
        end
        l = p:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        G = p:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if l and G and l.Health > 0 then
            pcall(function()
                G.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                G.CFrame = CFrame.new(G.Position.X, workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight - 500, G.Position.Z)
            end)
        end
    end)
    task.defer(function()
        pcall(function()
            C:LoadCharacter()
        end)
    end)
    if I then
        local z
        z = C.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
            if z then
                z:Disconnect()
            end
            task.defer(function()
                pcall(I)
            end)
        end)
    end
end
local function z(I, p)
    local l, G = pcall(function()
        if type(I) ~= "string" or I == "" then
            return p
        end
        local b = Enum.KeyCode[I]
        if b then
            return b
        end
        if #I == 1 then
            local CG = string.upper(I)
            b = Enum.KeyCode[CG]
            if b then
                return b
            end
        end
        return p
    end)
    return l and G or p
end

                    QG,
                    0.22,
                    { Position = JG and UDim2.new(1, -23.0, 0.5, -11.0) or UDim2.new(0, 1, 0.5, -11.0) }
                )
                g(rG, 0.22, { Transparency = JG and 0.85 or 0.6 })
                g(nG, 0.22, { Transparency = JG and 0.35 or 0.65 })
                g(aG, 0.22, { BackgroundTransparency = JG and 0 or 0.02 })
                if JG then
                    OG = true
                    fG()
                    g(eG, 0.22, { Transparency = 0.1 })
                else
                    OG = false
                    iG += 1
                    g(eG, 0.12, { Transparency = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine)
                end
            end)
        end)
        return { Set = UG, Container = aG }
    end
    local function g(rG, nG)
        local fG = Instance.new("Frame")
        fG.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, nG or N(46))

        fG.BackgroundTransparency = 0.12

        fG.Parent = (_G.HazeActiveScroll or I)
        b(fG, N(10))
        H(fG, 1, q.STROKE, 0.55)
        CG(fG, N(12), N(8), N(12), N(8))
        local H = Instance.new("TextLabel")

        H.Size = UDim2.new(1, -(N(67) + N(14)), 1, 0)

        H.TextSize = N(14)
        H.TextColor3 = q.TEXT
        H.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        H.Parent = fG

    end

        local A = g("Default To Nearest")
        local Z = w(A, i.DefaultToNearest, function(A, u)
            if A then
                i.DefaultToNearest = true
                i.DefaultToHighest = false
                i.DefaultToPriority = false
                u(true)
                if E.setFn then
                    E.setFn(false)
                end
                if m.setFn then
                    m.setFn(false)
                end
                i.AutoTPPriority = true
                if autoTPPriorityToggleRef and autoTPPriorityToggleRef.setFn then
                    autoTPPriorityToggleRef.setFn(true)
                end
            else
                local h = i.DefaultToHighest or i.DefaultToPriority
                if not h then
                    u(true)
                    F("DEFAULT MODE", "At least one default must be enabled")
                    return
                end
                i.DefaultToNearest = false
                u(false)
            end
            U()
            F("DEFAULT TO NEAREST", A and "ENABLED" or "DISABLED")
        end)
        O.setFn = Z.Set
        local A = g("Default To Highest")
        local Z = w(A, i.DefaultToHighest, function(A, u)
            if A then
                i.DefaultToNearest = false
                i.DefaultToHighest = true
                i.DefaultToPriority = false
                u(true)
                if O.setFn then
                    O.setFn(false)
                end
                if m.setFn then
                    m.setFn(false)
                end
                i.AutoTPPriority = false
                if autoTPPriorityToggleRef and autoTPPriorityToggleRef.setFn then
                    autoTPPriorityToggleRef.setFn(false)
                end
            else
                local h = i.DefaultToNearest or i.DefaultToPriority
                if not h then
                    u(true)
                    F("DEFAULT MODE", "At least one default must be enabled")
                    return
                end
                i.DefaultToHighest = false
                u(false)
            end
            U()
            F("DEFAULT TO HIGHEST", A and "ENABLED" or "DISABLED")
        end)
        E.setFn = Z.Set
        local A = g("Default To Priority")
        local Z = w(A, i.DefaultToPriority, function(A, u)
            if A then
                i.DefaultToNearest = false
                i.DefaultToHighest = false
                i.DefaultToPriority = true
                u(true)
                if O.setFn then
                    O.setFn(false)
                end
                if E.setFn then
                    E.setFn(false)
                end
                i.AutoTPPriority = true
                if autoTPPriorityToggleRef and autoTPPriorityToggleRef.setFn then
                    autoTPPriorityToggleRef.setFn(true)
                end
            else
                local O = i.DefaultToNearest or i.DefaultToHighest
                if not O then
                    u(true)
                    F("DEFAULT MODE", "At least one default must be enabled")
                    return
                end
                i.DefaultToPriority = false
                u(false)
            end
            U()
            F("DEFAULT TO PRIORITY", A and "ENABLED" or "DISABLED")
        end)
        m.setFn = Z.Set
    end

        if d then
            local O = {}
            O.row = g("Mobile GUI Scale")
            O.bg = Instance.new("Frame", O.row)

            O.fill.BackgroundColor3 = W.Accent1
            O.fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
            O.fill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
            Instance.new("UICorner", O.fill).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
            O.knob = Instance.new("Frame", O.bg)
            O.knob.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14)
            O.knob.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
            O.knob.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            O.knob.BorderSizePixel = 0
            Instance.new("UICorner", O.knob).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
            O.stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", O.knob)
            O.stroke.Color = W.Accent1
            O.stroke.Thickness = 2
            O.readout = Instance.new("TextLabel", O.row)
            O.readout.Size = UDim2.new(0, 50, 0, 20)
            O.readout.Position = UDim2.new(1, -145.0, 0.5, -10.0)
            O.readout.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            O.readout.Text = string.format("%.1f", i.MobileGuiScale or 0.5)
            O.readout.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            O.readout.TextSize = 11
            O.readout.TextColor3 = W.Accent1
            O.readout.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
            O.dragging = false
            O.min = 0.1
            O.max = 1
            O.scaleToT = function(q)
                return math.clamp((q - O.min) / (O.max - O.min), 0, 1)
            end
            O.updateScaleSlider = function(q)
                local w = math.clamp(q, O.min, O.max)
                i.MobileGuiScale = w
                U()
                O.fill.Size = UDim2.new(O.scaleToT(w), 0, 1, 0)
                O.knob.Position = UDim2.new(0, O.scaleToT(w) * O.bg.AbsoluteSize.X, 0.5, 0)
                O.readout.Text = string.format("%.2f", w)
                if P.RefreshMobileScale then
                    P.RefreshMobileScale()
                end
            end
            task.defer(function()
                O.updateScaleSlider(i.MobileGuiScale or 0.5)
            end)
            O.onScaleInput = function(q)
                O.updateScaleSlider(
                    O.min
                        + math.clamp((q.X - O.bg.AbsolutePosition.X) / O.bg.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, 1)
                            * (O.max - O.min)
                )
            end
            O.bg.InputBegan:Connect(function(q)
                if

        end

                V.Positions.Settings.OffsetY or 0
            )

                    V.Positions.AutoSteal.X,
                    V.Positions.AutoSteal.OffsetX or 0,
                    V.Positions.AutoSteal.Y,
                    V.Positions.AutoSteal.OffsetY or 0
                )
            end
            if x:FindFirstChild("XiAdminPanel") and x.XiAdminPanel:FindFirstChild("Frame") then
                x.XiAdminPanel.Frame.Position = UDim2.new(
                    V.Positions.AdminPanel.X,
                    V.Positions.AdminPanel.OffsetX or 0,
                    V.Positions.AdminPanel.Y,
                    V.Positions.AdminPanel.OffsetY or 0
                )
            end
            if x:FindFirstChild("XiInvisPanel") and x.XiInvisPanel:FindFirstChild("Frame") then
                x.XiInvisPanel.Frame.Position = UDim2.new(
                    V.Positions.InvisPanel.X,
                    V.Positions.InvisPanel.OffsetX or 0,
                    V.Positions.InvisPanel.Y,
                    V.Positions.InvisPanel.OffsetY or 0
                )

            then

                    V.Positions.TargetControls.X,
                    V.Positions.TargetControls.OffsetX or 0,
                    V.Positions.TargetControls.Y,
                    V.Positions.TargetControls.OffsetY or 0

            end

        end)
    end

local function V(O)
    if not O or type(O) ~= "string" then
        return 0
    end
    O = O:gsub("%s", ""):lower()
    if O == "" then
        return 0
    end
    local q, c = O:match("^([%d%.]+)([kmb]?)$")
    if not q then
        return 0
    end
    q = tonumber(q)
    if not q or q < 0 then
        return 0
    end
    if c == "k" then
        return q * 1000
    elseif c == "m" then
        return q * 1000000
    elseif c == "b" then
        return q * 1000000000
    end
    return q
end
if i.TpSettings.TpOnLoad then
    task.spawn(function()
        local O = V(i.TpSettings.MinGenForTp)
        local V = tostring(i.TpSettings.MinGenForTp or "")
        local function q(c)
            if not c then
                return false
            end
            if not c.name or not c.uid then
                return false
            end
            if c.owner == C.Name then
                return false
            end
            if O > 0 and (tonumber(c.genValue) or 0) < O then
                return false
            end
            return true
        end
        local function c()
            local Y = P and P.AllAnimalsCache
            if not Y or #Y == 0 then
                return nil
            end
            for I = 1, #X do
                local p = X[I]:lower()
                for I, I in ipairs(Y) do
                    if q(I) and I.name:lower() == p then
                        return I
                    end
                end
            end
            return nil
        end
        local function Y()
            local I = P and P.AllAnimalsCache
            if not I or #I == 0 then
                return nil
            end
            for p, p in ipairs(I) do
                if q(p) then
                    return p
                end
            end
            return nil
        end
        local function q()
            return c() or Y()
        end
        local c = nil
        local Y = false
        local I = os.clock() + 6
        repeat
            local p = P and P.AllAnimalsCache
            if p and #p > 0 then
                Y = true
            end
            c = q()
            if c then
                break
            end
            R.Heartbeat:Wait()
        until os.clock() >= I
        if not c then
            if O > 0 then
                F("MIN GEN", "No target >= " .. V .. " after cache refresh.")
            elseif Y then
                F("TIMEOUT", "No valid target found.")
            else
                F("TIMEOUT", "No target cache found.")
            end
            return
        end
        P.SelectedPetData = {
            petName = c.name,
            name = c.name,
            mpsText = c.genText,
            mpsValue = c.genValue,
            genText = c.genText,
            genValue = c.genValue,
            owner = c.owner,
            plot = c.plot,
            slot = c.slot,
            uid = c.uid,
            mutation = c.mutation,
            animalData = c,
        }
        if _G.tpToBestBrainrot then
            pcall(_G.tpToBestBrainrot)
        else
            B(0.1)
            S()
        end
    end)
end

            _G.toggleInvisibleSteal()

        end

            _G.toggleInvisibleSteal()
        end
    end
end)
task.spawn(function()
    local J = false
    local V = i.StealSpeed or 25.5
    local O = nil
    local function q()
        J = false
        if O then
            O:Disconnect()
            O = nil
        end
    end
    P.GetStealSpeed = function()
        return V
    end
    P.SetStealSpeed = function(c, B)
        local Y = tonumber(c)
        if not Y then
            return V
        end
        V = math.clamp(Y, 5, 100)
        i.StealSpeed = V
        if not B then
            U()
        end
        if P._ssUpdateBtn then
            P._ssUpdateBtn()
        end
        return V
    end
    P.DisableStealSpeed = function()
        q()
        if P._ssUpdateBtn then
            P._ssUpdateBtn()
        end
    end
    local function c()
        J = true
        if O then
            O:Disconnect()
            O = nil
        end
        O = R.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            local O = C.Character
            if not O then
                return
            end
            local B = O:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local Y = O:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not B or not Y then
                return
            end
            local O = B.MoveDirection
            if O.Magnitude > 0 then
                Y.Velocity = Vector3.new(O.X * V, Y.Velocity.Y, O.Z * V)
            end
        end)
    end
    P._ssUpdateBtn = function() end
    P.StealSpeedToggleFunc = function()
        if J then
            q()
        else
            c()
        end
        if P._ssUpdateBtn then
            P._ssUpdateBtn()
        end
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        local V = nil
        while true do
            task.wait(0.3)
            if not i.AutoStealSpeed then
                V = nil
                continue
            end
            local O = C:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true
            if V == O then
                continue
            end
            V = O
            if O and not J then
                c()
                if P._ssUpdateBtn then
                    P._ssUpdateBtn()
                end
            elseif not O and J then
                q()
                if P._ssUpdateBtn then
                    P._ssUpdateBtn()
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end)

        local c = P and P.AllAnimalsCache
        if type(c) ~= "table" then
            return ""
        end
        local B = {}
        for Y, I in ipairs(c) do
            if I and I.uid then
                B[Y] = tostring(I.uid) .. (I.genText or "")
            end
        end
        return table.concat(B, "|")
    end

                    B:Destroy()
                end)
            end
        end

            if B:IsA("BillboardGui") and string.sub(B.Name, 1, 12) == "BrainrotESP_" then
                pcall(function()
                    B:Destroy()
                end)
            end
        end
    end
    c()
    local c = Instance.new("Folder")
    c.Name = "XiBrainrotESP"
    c.Parent = a
    local B = {}
    local Y = game:GetService("CoreGui")
    local Y = 0
    local I = nil
    local p = nil
    local G = C.DisplayName
    local w = {
        Cursed = Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 50),
        Gold = Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 0),
        Diamond = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 255),
        YinYang = Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 220),
        Rainbow = Color3.fromRGB(255, 100, 200),
        Lava = Color3.fromRGB(255, 100, 20),
        Candy = Color3.fromRGB(255, 105, 180),
        Bloodrot = Color3.fromRGB(139, 0, 0),
        Radioactive = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0),
        Divine = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
    }
    local function b(E)
        if not E then
            return nil
        end
        local m = E:GetAttribute("_BR_UID")
        if m then
            return tostring(m)
        end
        Y += 1
        local m = "br_" .. tostring(Y)
        E:SetAttribute("_BR_UID", m)
        return m
    end
    local function Y(E)
        E = tostring(E or "")
        if E == "" then
            return nil
        end
        E = E:gsub("%s+", "")
        local m, A = E:match("%$?([%d%.]+)([KkMmBb]?)/s")
        if not m then
            return nil
        end
        local E = tonumber(m)
        if not E then
            return nil
        end
        A = (A or ""):upper()
        if A == "B" then
            return E * 1000, true
        elseif A == "M" then
            return E, false
        elseif A == "K" then
            return E / 1000, false
        else
            return E / 1000000, false
        end
    end
    local function E(m)
        local A = m or 0
        if A >= 1000 then
            local m = A / 1000
            if math.floor(m) == m then
                return string.format("$%dB/s", m)
            else
                return string.format("$%.1fB/s", m)
            end
        elseif A >= 1 then
            if math.floor(A) == A then
                return string.format("$%dM/s", A)
            else
                return string.format("$%.1fM/s", A)
            end
        elseif A >= 0.001 then
            local m = A * 1000
            if math.floor(m) == m then
                return string.format("$%dK/s", m)
            else
                return string.format("$%.1fK/s", m)
            end
        else
            local m = math.floor(A * 1000000 + 0.5)
            return string.format("$%d/s", m)
        end
    end
    local function m()
        local A = a:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        if not A then
            return nil
        end
        for H, H in ipairs(A:GetChildren()) do
            local A = H:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
            if A then
                local Z = A:FindFirstChild("SurfaceGui")
                if Z then
                    local A = Z:FindFirstChild("Frame")
                    if A then
                        local Z = A:FindFirstChild("TextLabel")
                        if Z and Z:IsA("TextLabel") then
                            local A = tostring(Z.Text or "")
                            if A:find(G, 1, true) and A:find("'s Base", 1, true) then
                                return H
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return nil
    end
    local function G(A)
        if not A then
            return nil
        end
        local H, Z, k = math.huge, math.huge, math.huge
        local u, g, h = -math.huge, -math.huge, -math.huge
        local L = false
        for s, s in ipairs(A:GetDescendants()) do
            if s:IsA("BasePart") then
                L = true
                local A = s.Position
                local N = s.Size / 2
                local s, PG = A.X - N.X, A.X + N.X
                local CG, rG = A.Y - N.Y, A.Y + N.Y
                local RG, dG = A.Z - N.Z, A.Z + N.Z
                if s < H then
                    H = s
                end
                if CG < Z then
                    Z = CG
                end
                if RG < k then
                    k = RG
                end
                if PG > u then
                    u = PG
                end
                if rG > g then
                    g = rG
                end
                if dG > h then
                    h = dG
                end
            end
        end
        if not L then
            return nil
        end
        return { minX = H, maxX = u, minY = Z, maxY = g, minZ = k, maxZ = h }
    end
    local function A(A, H, Z)
        if not H then
            return false
        end

            and A.X <= H.maxX + Z
            and A.Y >= H.minY - Z
            and A.Y <= H.maxY + Z
            and A.Z >= H.minZ - Z
            and A.Z <= H.maxZ + Z
    end

        w.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6.0, 0, 10)
        w.Position = UDim2.new(0, 3, 1, -12.0)
        w.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        w.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        w.TextSize = 9
        w.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
        w.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(210, 210, 210)
        w.Text = ""
        return m
    end
    local function w(m, H, Z)
        if not m or not m.Parent then
            return
        end
        local k = m:FindFirstChild("Container")
        if not k then
            return
        end
        local u = k:FindFirstChild("Stroke")
        local g = k:FindFirstChild("Tag")
        local h = k:FindFirstChild("Name")
        local L = k:FindFirstChild("Gen")
        local s = k:FindFirstChild("Info")
        if H and H.kind == "CONVEYOR" then
            if u then
                u.Color = hasMutation and color or Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 60)
            end
            if g then
                g.Visible = true
                g.Text = "[CARPET]"
                g.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 150)
            end
            if h then
                h.Text = tostring(H.name or H.petName or "Brainrot")
            end
            if L then
                L.Text = tostring(H.genText or "")
            end
            if s then
                if Z and H.adornee and H.adornee.Parent then
                    local k = math.floor((Z.Position - H.adornee.Position).Magnitude)
                    s.Text = tostring(k) .. " studs"
                else
                    s.Text = ""
                end
            end
            m.Adornee = H.adornee
            return
        end
        local k = H.mutation
        local N = k and k ~= "" and k ~= "None" and k ~= "N/A"
        local PG = p(k)
        if u then
            u.Color = N and PG or Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 60)
        end
        if g then
            g.Visible = N
            g.Text = N and string.upper(tostring(k)) or ""
            g.TextColor3 = PG
        end
        if h then
            h.Text = tostring(H.name or H.petName or "Brainrot")
            h.TextWrapped = true
            h.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None
            h.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
            h.Position = N and UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 12) or UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 8)
            h.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6.0, 0, 24)
        end
        if L then
            L.Text = tostring(H.genText or "")
            L.Position = N and UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 39) or UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 36)
        end
        if s then
            if Z and H.adornee and H.adornee.Parent then
                local p = math.floor((Z.Position - H.adornee.Position).Magnitude)
                s.Text = tostring(p) .. " studs"
            else
                s.Text = ""
            end
        end
        m.Adornee = H.adornee
    end
    local function p()
        for m, H in pairs(B) do
            if H.bb then
                pcall(function()
                    H.bb:Destroy()
                end)
            end
            B[m] = nil
        end
        if c and c.Parent then
            for m, m in ipairs(c:GetChildren()) do
                pcall(function()
                    m:Destroy()
                end)
            end
        end
        for m, m in ipairs(a:GetDescendants()) do
            if m:IsA("BillboardGui") and string.sub(m.Name, 1, 12) == "BrainrotESP_" then
                pcall(function()
                    m:Destroy()
                end)
            end
        end
    end
    local m = {
        STOLEN = true,
        STEAL = true,
        PURCHASE = true,
        COMPRAR = true,
        BUY = true,
        COLLECT = true,
        COLETAR = true,
        CASH = true,
        VALUE = true,
        BASE = true,
        EMPTY = true,
        GENERATION = true,
        COMMON = true,
        UNCOMMON = true,
        RARE = true,
        EPIC = true,
        LEGENDARY = true,
        DIVINE = true,
        RAINBOW = true,
        CURSED = true,
        GOLD = true,
        DIAMOND = true,
        CANDY = true,
        MUTATION = true,
    }
    local function H(Z)
        if not Z or Z == "" then
            return false
        end
        local k = (Z:gsub("<[^>]+>", "")):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") or ""
        if #k <= 1 then
            return false
        end
        local Z = k:upper()
        if Z:find("^%$") or Z:find("/S$") or Z:find("^[%d%.]+") then
            return false
        end
        return not m[Z]
    end
    local function m(Z)
        if type(Z) ~= "string" then
            return nil
        end
        local k = Z:gsub("<[^>]+>", ""):upper()
        if not k:find("%$") or not k:find("/S") then
            return nil
        end
        local Z = k:gsub("%$", ""):gsub("/S", ""):gsub("%s+", "")
        local k = tonumber(Z:match("[%d%.]+"))
        if not k then
            return nil
        end
        if Z:find("B") then
            return k * 1000000000
        elseif Z:find("M") then
            return k * 1000000
        elseif Z:find("K") then
            return k * 1000
        else
            return k
        end
    end
    local function Z(k)
        if not k then
            return nil, nil, 0
        end
        local u, g, h = nil, nil, 0
        for L, L in ipairs(k:GetDescendants()) do
            if L:IsA("BillboardGui") or L:IsA("SurfaceGui") then
                for k, k in ipairs(L:GetDescendants()) do
                    if k:IsA("TextLabel") and k.Text then
                        local L = m(k.Text)
                        if L and L > h then
                            h = L
                            g = k.Text:gsub("<[^>]+>", "")
                            local m = k.Parent
                            if m then
                                local k = nil
                                for L, L in ipairs(m:GetChildren()) do
                                    if L:IsA("TextLabel") and L.Name == "DisplayName" then
                                        local s = (L.Text or ""):gsub("<[^>]+>", ""):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
                                        if H(s) then
                                            k = s
                                            break
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                                if not k then
                                    local L, s = nil, 0
                                    for N, N in ipairs(m:GetChildren()) do
                                        if N:IsA("TextLabel") then
                                            local m = (N.Text or ""):gsub("<[^>]+>", ""):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
                                                or ""
                                            if H(m) and #m > s then
                                                L, s = m, #m
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                    if L then
                                        k = L
                                    end
                                end
                                if k then
                                    u = k
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return u, g, h
    end
    local m = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
    local H = {}
    local function k(u)
        if not u or not u:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
            return false
        end
        local g = string.lower(tostring(u.ActionText or ""))
        return g:find("purchase", 1, true) or g:find("comprar", 1, true) or g:find("buy", 1, true)
    end
    local function u(g)
        if k(g) then
            m[g] = true
        end
    end
    do
        for m, m in ipairs(a:GetDescendants()) do
            if m:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
                u(m)
            end
        end
    end
    a.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(m)
        if m:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
            u(m)
        end
    end)
    local function m()
        local k = {}
        local u = a:FindFirstChild("Debris") or a
        for g, g in ipairs(u:GetChildren()) do
            if g:IsA("Model") or g:IsA("BasePart") then
                local u, h, L = Z(g)
                if L and L >= 10000000 then
                    local s = g:IsA("BasePart") and g or g:IsA("Model") and g.PrimaryPart
                    if not s and g:IsA("Model") then
                        for N, N in ipairs(g:GetChildren()) do
                            if N:IsA("BasePart") then
                                s = N
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    if s then
                        table.insert(k, { name = u, gen = h, gv = L, part = s, model = g })
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return k
    end
    local function k()
        local u = m()
        local m = {}
        local g = {}
        for h, h in ipairs(a:GetDescendants()) do
            if h:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and h.Enabled then
                local L = string.lower(tostring(h.ActionText or ""))
                if L:find("purchase", 1, true) or L:find("comprar", 1, true) or L:find("buy", 1, true) then
                    local L = h.Parent
                    if L and L:IsA("Attachment") then
                        L = L.Parent
                    end
                    if L and L:IsA("BasePart") then
                        local s, N, PG, CG = "Brainrot", "", 0, nil
                        local rG, RG = 15, nil
                        for dG, dG in ipairs(u) do
                            if dG.part and dG.part.Parent then
                                local u = (dG.part.Position - L.Position).Magnitude
                                if u < rG then
                                    rG = u
                                    RG = dG
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        if RG then
                            s = RG.name or "Brainrot"
                            N = RG.gen or ""
                            PG = RG.gv or 0
                            CG = RG.model
                        else
                            local u = L
                            local rG = L
                            while rG and rG.Parent and rG.Parent ~= a do
                                u = rG
                                rG = rG.Parent
                            end
                            local rG, RG, dG = Z(u)
                            if dG and dG >= 10000000 then
                                s = rG or s
                                N = RG or N
                                PG = dG
                                CG = u
                            end
                        end
                        if PG >= 10000000 then
                            local Z = b(L) or tostring(h)
                            local b = "conv_" .. tostring(Z)
                            if not g[b] then
                                g[b] = true
                                table.insert(
                                    m,
                                    {
                                        uid = b,
                                        kind = "CONVEYOR",
                                        name = s,
                                        petName = s,
                                        genText = N,
                                        genValue = PG,
                                        mutation = "None",
                                        adornee = L,
                                        model = CG,
                                    }
                                )
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        table.sort(m, function(b, Z)
            return (tonumber(b.genValue) or 0) > (tonumber(Z.genValue) or 0)
        end)
        local b = {}
        for Z = 1, math.min(3, #m) do
            b[Z] = m[Z]
        end
        return b
    end
    local function b()
        local m = {}
        local Z = P and P.AllAnimalsCache
        if type(Z) ~= "table" then
            return m
        end
        for u, u in ipairs(Z) do
            if u and u.uid and u.plot and u.slot then
                if not I or tostring(u.plot) ~= tostring(I.Name) then
                    local I = M(u)
                    if I and I:IsA("BasePart") and I.Parent then
                        local M = select(1, Y(u.genText or ""))
                        local Y = tonumber(u.genValue) or (M or 0) * 1000000
                        if M and M >= 35 or Y >= 35000000 then
                            table.insert(
                                m,
                                {
                                    uid = "plot_" .. tostring(u.uid),
                                    kind = "PLOT",
                                    name = u.name,
                                    petName = u.name,
                                    genText = u.genText or E((Y or 0) / 1000000),
                                    genValue = Y,
                                    mutation = u.mutation or "None",
                                    adornee = I,
                                    owner = u.owner,
                                    plot = u.plot,
                                    slot = u.slot,
                                }
                            )
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        table.sort(m, function(Y, I)
            return (tonumber(Y.genValue) or 0) > (tonumber(I.genValue) or 0)
        end)
        return m
    end
    local function Y(I)
        local M = {}
        local E = C.Character
        local m = E and E:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local E = m and m.Position or Vector3.zero
        for Z, Z in ipairs(I) do
            if Z and Z.uid and Z.adornee and Z.adornee.Parent then
                M[Z.uid] = true
                local I = B[Z.uid]
                local u = not I or not I.bb or not I.bb.Parent or I.adornee ~= Z.adornee
                if u then
                    if I and I.bb then
                        pcall(function()
                            I.bb:Destroy()
                        end)
                    end
                    local u = G(Z)
                    u.Adornee = Z.adornee
                    u.Parent = c
                    B[Z.uid] = { bb = u, adornee = Z.adornee, kind = Z.kind, lastHash = "" }
                    I = B[Z.uid]
                else
                    I.adornee = Z.adornee
                    I.kind = Z.kind
                end
                local c = m and Z.adornee and math.floor((E - Z.adornee.Position).Magnitude) or 0
                local G = tostring(Z.genText) .. tostring(Z.mutation) .. tostring(Z.name) .. tostring(c)
                if I.lastHash ~= G then
                    I.lastHash = G
                    w(I.bb, Z, m)
                end
            end
        end
        for c, I in pairs(B) do
            if not M[c] then
                if I.bb then
                    pcall(function()
                        I.bb:Destroy()
                    end)
                end
                B[c] = nil
            end
        end
    end
    local function c()
        if not J then
            return
        end
        local I = C.Character
        local G = I and I:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local I = G and G.Position or Vector3.zero
        local I = q()
        if I ~= V then
            V = I
            local V = {}
            local q = b()
            for I = 1, #q do
                V[#V + 1] = q[I]
            end
            for q = 1, #H do
                V[#V + 1] = H[q]
            end
            table.sort(V, function(q, I)
                return (tonumber(q.genValue) or 0) > (tonumber(I.genValue) or 0)
            end)
            O = V
        end
        Y(O)
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            A()
            task.wait(2)
        end
    end)
    if not espToggleRef then
        espToggleRef = { enabled = true, setFn = nil }
    end
    espToggleRef.setFn = function(V)
        J = V
        i.BrainrotESP = V
        if V then
            task.spawn(function()
                A()
                pcall(function()
                    H = k()
                end)
                pcall(c)
            end)
        else
            p()
        end
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            task.wait(1.2)
            if J then
                pcall(function()
                    H = k()
                end)
            end
        end
    end)
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            task.wait(2)
            if J then
                pcall(c)
            end
        end
    end)
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            task.wait(2)
            if J and next(B) == nil then
                pcall(function()
                    H = k()
                end)
                pcall(c)
            end
        end
    end)
end)

    local function M()
        local w = C.Character
        if w and w:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and w.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
            c = w.Humanoid.HipHeight
            q = w:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                return false

            for E, E in pairs(q:GetChildren()) do

                        A.Part0 = O
                    end
                    if A.Part1 == q then
                        A.Part1 = O
                    end
                end
            end
            E:Destroy()

        end
        return false
    end
    local function w()

        if not q or not q:IsDescendantOf(a) or not E or E.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
