        A.Parent = game
        E.Parent = A
        q.Parent = E
        E.PrimaryPart = q
        E.Parent = a
        q.CanCollide = true
        for A, A in pairs(E:GetDescendants()) do
            if A:IsA("Weld") or A:IsA("Motor6D") then
                if A.Part0 == O then
                    A.Part0 = q
                end
                if A.Part1 == O then
                    A.Part1 = q
                end
            end
        end
        if O then
            local A = O.CFrame
            O:Destroy()
            O = nil
            q.CFrame = A
        end
        q = nil
        if E and E.Humanoid then
            E.Humanoid.HipHeight = c
        end
        G()
    end
    local function c()
        local E = C.Character
        if E and E:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and E.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
            local A = Instance.new("Animation")
            A.AnimationId = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=18537363391"
            local H = E.Humanoid
            local E = H:FindFirstChild("Animator") or Instance.new("Animator", H)
            local H = E:LoadAnimation(A)
            H.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
            H:Play(0, 1, 0)
            A:Destroy()
            table.insert(V, H)
            H.Stopped:Connect(function()
                if J then
                    c()
                end
            end)
            task.delay(0, function()
                H.TimePosition = 0.7
                task.spawn(function()
                    task.wait(0.3)
                    if H then
                        H:AdjustSpeed(math.huge)
                    end
                end)
            end)
        end
    end
    local function E()
        G()
        if not J then
            return
        end
        local A = C.Character
        local H = A and A:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        J = false
        _G.invisibleStealEnabled = false
        for A, A in pairs(V) do
            pcall(function()
                A:Stop()
            end)
        end
        V = {}
        if B then
            B:Disconnect()
            B = nil
        end
        for A, A in ipairs(Y) do
            if A then
                A:Disconnect()
            end
        end
        Y = {}
        w()
        G()
        if H then
            pcall(function()
                H:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
            end)
        end
        if _G.updateMovementPanelInvisVisual then
            pcall(_G.updateMovementPanelInvisVisual, false)
        end
        if updateVisualState then
            updateVisualState(false)
        end
    end
    local function Y()
        if J then
            return
        end
        local w = C.Character
        if not w then
            return
        end
        local A = w:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not A then
            return
        end
        J = true
        _G.invisibleStealEnabled = true
        if _G.updateMovementPanelInvisVisual then
            pcall(_G.updateMovementPanelInvisVisual, true)
        end
        if updateVisualState then
            updateVisualState(true)
        end
        V = {}
        I()
        local V = M()
        if V then
            task.wait(0.05)
            c()
            task.defer(function()
                if _G.resetBrainrotBeam then
                    pcall(_G.resetBrainrotBeam)
                end
                if _G.resetPlotBeam then
                    pcall(_G.resetPlotBeam)
                end
                task.wait(0.1)
                if _G.updateBrainrotBeam then
                    pcall(_G.updateBrainrotBeam)
                end
                if _G.createPlotBeam then
                    pcall(_G.createPlotBeam)
                end
            end)
            local V = nil
            local c = 5
            B = R.PreSimulation:Connect(function()
                if w and w:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and w.Humanoid.Health > 0 and q then
                    local B = w.PrimaryPart or w:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if B then
                        if c > 0 then
                            c = c - 1
                            V = nil
                        elseif V and true then
                            local c = q.Position
                            local I = (c - V).Magnitude
                            if I > 3 and not _G.RecoveryInProgress then
                                V = nil
                                b(c)
                                if _G.AutoRecoverLagback and _G.toggleInvisibleSteal then
                                    _G.RecoveryInProgress = true
                                    task.spawn(function()
                                        pcall(_G.toggleInvisibleSteal)
                                        task.wait(0.5)
                                        pcall(_G.toggleInvisibleSteal)
                                        _G.RecoveryInProgress = false
                                    end)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        if O then
                            O.CanCollide = false
                        end
                        for c, c in pairs(q:GetChildren()) do
                            if c:IsA("Attachment") or c:IsA("Beam") then
                                c:Destroy()
                            end
                        end
                        local c = _G.InvisStealAngle or 180
                        local I = (_G.SinkSliderValue or 5) * 0.5
                        local M = B.CFrame - Vector3.new(0, I, 0)
                        q.CFrame = M * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(c), 0, 0)
                        q.Velocity = B.Velocity
                        q.CanCollide = false
                        V = q.Position
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end

        local dG = L
        local function nG(fG)
            if s and s < 1 then
                return string.format("%.2f", fG)
            end
            if math.abs(fG - math.floor(fG)) < 0.001 then
                return tostring(math.floor(fG + 0.5))
            end
            return string.format("%.1f", fG)
        end
        local function fG(JG, aG)
            dG = math.clamp(JG, g, h)
            if s and s > 0 then
                dG = math.floor(dG / s + 0.5) * s
                dG = math.clamp(dG, g, h)
            end
            local s = (dG - g) / (h - g)
            rG.Size = UDim2.new(s, 0, 1, 0)
            RG.Position = UDim2.new(s, 0, 0.5, 0)
            u.Text = nG(dG)
            if N and not aG then
                N(dG)
            end
        end
        local function u(s)
            local N = CG.AbsolutePosition.X
            local rG = CG.AbsoluteSize.X
            local RG = math.clamp((s.Position.X - N) / rG, 0, 1)
            local s = g + (h - g) * RG
            fG(s, false)
        end

        end)

                fG(k, u)
            end,
            Get = function()
                return dG
            end,
        }
    end

task.spawn(function()
    local J = false
    local V = false
    local O = false
    task.wait(1)
    while task.wait(0.1) do
        if _G.AutoInvisDuringSteal == false then
            J = false
            O = false
        else
            local q = C:GetAttribute("Stealing")
            if q and not J then
                V = _G.invisibleStealEnabled or false
                if not _G.invisibleStealEnabled and _G.toggleInvisibleSteal then
                    task.delay(0.25, function()
                        if C:GetAttribute("Stealing") and not _G.invisibleStealEnabled then
                            pcall(_G.toggleInvisibleSteal)
                            O = true
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
            if not q and O and _G.invisibleStealEnabled and _G.toggleInvisibleSteal then
                pcall(_G.toggleInvisibleSteal)
                O = false
            end
            J = q
        end
    end
end)
task.spawn(function()
    local function J()
        local V = C.Character or C.CharacterAdded:Wait()
        local O = V:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local q = V:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
        return V, O, q
    end
    local function V(O)
        for q, q in ipairs(O:GetDescendants()) do
            if q:IsA("BillboardGui") then
                local O = q:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel", true)
                if O and O.Text:find("!") then
                    return true
                end
            end
        end
        return false
    end
    local function O(q)
        for c, c in ipairs(q:GetDescendants()) do
            if c:IsA("BasePart") and c ~= q then
                c.Transparency = 0.5
                c.CanCollide = false
                c.CanTouch = false
                c.CanQuery = false
            elseif c:IsA("BillboardGui") and c.Name ~= "SentryLabel" then
                c:Destroy()
            elseif c:IsA("Decal") or c:IsA("Texture") then
                c.Transparency = 0.5
            end
        end
        if q:IsA("BasePart") and q.Name ~= "ProxyVisual" then
            q.Transparency = 1
            q.CanCollide = false
        end
    end
    local function q()
        local c, c = J()
        local B, Y = nil, math.huge
        for I, I in ipairs(a:GetDescendants()) do
            if I.Name:match("^Sentry_") then
                if V(I) then
                    local p = I:IsA("BasePart") and I or I:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
                    if p then
                        local G = (c.Position - p.Position).Magnitude
                        if G < Y then
                            Y = G
                            B = I
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return B
    end
    while true do
        if i.AutoDestroyTurrets then
            if C:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true then
                task.wait(0.5)
            else
                local c = q()
                if c then
                    while c and c.Parent and C:GetAttribute("Stealing") ~= true do
                        local q, B, Y = J()
                        local J = C.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Bat") or q:FindFirstChild("Bat")
                        O(c)
                        local O = B.CFrame.LookVector * 4
                        local I = CFrame.new(B.Position + O, B.Position)
                        if c:IsA("Model") then
                            c:PivotTo(I)
                        elseif c:IsA("BasePart") then
                            c.CFrame = I
                        end
                        if J then
                            if J.Parent ~= q then
                                Y:EquipTool(J)
                            end
                            J:Activate()
                        end
                        task.wait(0.1)
                        if not V(c) then
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        task.wait(0.1)
    end
end)
P.FOV_MANAGER = { activeCount = 0, conn = nil, forcedFOV = 70 }
function P.FOV_MANAGER:Start()
    if self.conn then
        return
    end
    self.forcedFOV = i.FOV or 70
    self.conn = R.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local J = a.CurrentCamera
        if J then
            local V = i.FOV or self.forcedFOV
            if J.FieldOfView ~= V then
                J.FieldOfView = V
            end
        end
    end)
end
function P.FOV_MANAGER:Stop()
    if self.conn then
        self.conn:Disconnect()
        self.conn = nil
    end
end
function P.FOV_MANAGER:Push()
    self.activeCount = self.activeCount + 1
    self:Start()
end
function P.FOV_MANAGER:Pop()
    if self.activeCount > 0 then
        self.activeCount = self.activeCount - 1
    end
    if self.activeCount == 0 then
        self:Stop()
    end
end
P.ANTI_BEE_DISCO = {
    running = false,
    connections = {},
    originalMoveFunction = nil,
    controlsProtected = false,
    badLightingNames = { Blue = true, DiscoEffect = true, BeeBlur = true, ColorCorrection = true },
}
function P.ANTI_BEE_DISCO.nuke(J)
    if not J or not J.Parent then
        return
    end
    if P.ANTI_BEE_DISCO.badLightingNames[J.Name] then
        pcall(function()
            J:Destroy()
        end)
    end
end
function P.ANTI_BEE_DISCO.disconnectAll()
    for J, J in ipairs(P.ANTI_BEE_DISCO.connections) do
        if typeof(J) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
            J:Disconnect()
        end
    end
    P.ANTI_BEE_DISCO.connections = {}
end
function P.ANTI_BEE_DISCO.protectControls()
    if P.ANTI_BEE_DISCO.controlsProtected then
        return
    end
    pcall(function()
        local J = C.PlayerScripts
        local V = J:FindFirstChild("PlayerModule")
        if not V then
            return
        end
        local J = require(V):GetControls()
        if not J then
            return
        end
        local V = P.ANTI_BEE_DISCO
        if not V.originalMoveFunction then
            V.originalMoveFunction = J.moveFunction
        end
        local function O(q, c, B)
            if V.originalMoveFunction then
                V.originalMoveFunction(q, c, B)
            end
        end
        table.insert(
            V.connections,
            R.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if not V.running or not i.AntiBeeDisco then
                    return
                end
                local q = os.clock()
                if q - (V._lastCtrlTick or 0) < 0.2 then
                    return
                end
                V._lastCtrlTick = q
                if J.moveFunction ~= O then
                    J.moveFunction = O
                end
            end)
        )
        J.moveFunction = O
        V.controlsProtected = true
    end)
end
function P.ANTI_BEE_DISCO.restoreControls()
    if not P.ANTI_BEE_DISCO.controlsProtected then
        return
    end
    pcall(function()
        local J = C.PlayerScripts:FindFirstChild("PlayerModule")
        if not J then
            return
        end
        local V = require(J):GetControls()
        local J = P.ANTI_BEE_DISCO
        if V and J.originalMoveFunction then
            V.moveFunction = J.originalMoveFunction
            J.controlsProtected = false
        end
    end)
end
function P.ANTI_BEE_DISCO.blockBuzzingSound()
    pcall(function()
        local J = C.PlayerScripts:FindFirstChild("Bee", true)
        if J then
            local V = J:FindFirstChild("Buzzing")
            if V and V:IsA("Sound") then
                V:Stop()
                V.Volume = 0
            end
        end
    end)
end
function P.ANTI_BEE_DISCO.Enable()
    local J = P.ANTI_BEE_DISCO
    if J.running then
        return
    end
    J.running = true
    for V, V in ipairs(_:GetDescendants()) do
        J.nuke(V)
    end
    table.insert(
        J.connections,
        _.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(V)
            if not J.running or not i.AntiBeeDisco then
                return
            end
            J.nuke(V)
        end)
    )
    J.protectControls()
    table.insert(
        J.connections,
        R.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not J.running or not i.AntiBeeDisco then
                return
            end
            local V = os.clock()
            if V - (J._lastBuzzTick or 0) < 0.5 then
                return
            end
            J._lastBuzzTick = V
            J.blockBuzzingSound()
        end)
    )
    P.FOV_MANAGER:Push()
    F("ANTI-BEE & DISCO", "Enabled")
end
function P.ANTI_BEE_DISCO.Disable()
    local J = P.ANTI_BEE_DISCO
    if not J.running then
        return
    end
    J.running = false
    J.restoreControls()
    J.disconnectAll()
    P.FOV_MANAGER:Pop()
    F("ANTI-BEE & DISCO", "Disabled")
end
_G.ANTI_BEE_DISCO = P.ANTI_BEE_DISCO
if i.AntiBeeDisco then
    task.spawn(function()
        task.wait(1)
        if P.ANTI_BEE_DISCO.Enable then
            P.ANTI_BEE_DISCO.Enable()
        end
    end)
end
local function J(V)
    if V and i.FOV and V.FieldOfView ~= i.FOV then
        V.FieldOfView = i.FOV
    end
end
local function V(O)
    if not O then
        return
    end
    J(O)
    O:GetPropertyChangedSignal("FieldOfView"):Connect(function()
        J(O)
    end)
end
V(a.CurrentCamera)
a:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CurrentCamera"):Connect(function()
    V(a.CurrentCamera)
end)

        local z = math.abs(q)
        local B = math.max(1, z)
        local z = math.floor(math.log(B, 1000))
        local B = Q[z + 1] or "e+" .. z
        local Q = q * (10 ^ c / 1000 ^ z)
        local q = math.floor(Q) / 10 ^ c
        return (("%." .. c .. "f"):format(q)):gsub("%.?0+$", "") .. B
    end
    local function Q(q, c, z)
        local B = n[q]
        if not B then
            return 0
        end
        local q = B.Generation or B.Price * 0.1
        local B = 1
        if c and c ~= "None" then
            local Y = J[c]
            if Y and Y.Modifier then
                B = B + Y.Modifier
            end
        end
        local J = false
        if type(z) == "table" then
            for c, c in ipairs(z) do
                if c == "Sleepy" then
                    J = true
                else
                    local z = V[c]
                    if z and z.MultiplierModifier then
                        B = B + z.MultiplierModifier
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        local V = math.round(q * B)
        if J then
            V = math.round(V * 0.5)
        end
        return V
    end
    local J = false
    local V = {}
    local q = 0
    local c = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
    local function z()
        for B, B in ipairs(a.Plots:GetChildren()) do
            local Y = _getPlotData(B.Name)
            if Y and _isMyPlot(Y.Owner) then
                return B
            end
        end
        return nil
    end
    local function B(Y)
        local I = {}
        if not Y then
            return I
        end
        local p = _getPlotData(Y.Name)
        local Y = p and p.AnimalList or nil
        if not Y then
            return I
        end
        for p, l in pairs(Y) do
            if type(l) == "table" then
                I[p] = { Index = l.Index, Mutation = l.Mutation, Traits = l.Traits }
            end
        end
        return I
    end
    local function Y(I)
        local p = n[I.Index]
        local n = p and p.DisplayName or I.Index
        local p = Q(I.Index, I.Mutation, I.Traits)
        local Q = "$" .. O(p) .. "/s"
        return n, Q, I.Mutation
    end
    C:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Stealing"):Connect(function()
        local n = C:GetAttribute("Stealing")
        if n then
            J = true
            V = B(z())
            q = tick()
            local n = C.Character
            local Q = n and n:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if Q then
                c = Q.Position
            end
        else
            if not J then
                return
            end
            J = false
            local n = tick() - q
            local J = 0
            local Q = C.Character
            local O = Q and Q:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if O then
                J = (O.Position - c).Magnitude
            end
            task.wait(0.6)
            local Q = B(z())
            local O = nil
            for q, c in pairs(Q) do
                local Q = V[q]
                if not Q or (Q.Index ~= c.Index or Q.Mutation ~= c.Mutation) then
                    O = c
                    break
                end
            end
            if O then
                local Q, Q, Q = Y(O)
            else
                if i.AutoTpOnFailedSteal and n > 3 and J > 60 then
                    F("STEAL FAILED", string.format("Auto TPing... (%.1fs, %d studs)", n, J))
                    task.spawn(S)
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end)
P.FPSFunctions = {}
P.FPSFunctions.removeMeshes = function(n)
    if not n:IsA("Tool") then
        return
    end
    local J = n:FindFirstChild("Handle")
    if not J then
        return
    end
    local n = J:GetDescendants()
    for J = 1, #n do
        local Q = n[J]
        if Q:IsA("SpecialMesh") or Q:IsA("Mesh") or Q:IsA("FileMesh") then
            Q:Destroy()
        end
    end
end
P.FPSFunctions.onCharacterAdded = function(n)
    local J = P.FPSFunctions
    n.ChildAdded:Connect(function(Q)
        if Q:IsA("Tool") and i.FPSBoost then
            J.removeMeshes(Q)
        end
    end)
    local Q = n:GetChildren()
    for n = 1, #Q do
        if Q[n]:IsA("Tool") then
            J.removeMeshes(Q[n])
        end
    end
end
P.FPSFunctions.onPlayerAdded = function(n)
    local J = P.FPSFunctions
    n.CharacterAdded:Connect(J.onCharacterAdded)
    if n.Character then
        J.onCharacterAdded(n.Character)
    end
end
P.FPSFunctions.initPlayerTracking = function()
    local n = P.FPSFunctions
    local J = r:GetPlayers()
    for Q = 1, #J do
        n.onPlayerAdded(J[Q])
    end
    r.PlayerAdded:Connect(n.onPlayerAdded)
end
P.FPSFunctions.initPlayerTracking()

do

        { "balloon", "inverse", "jail", "jumpscare", "morph", "nightvision", "ragdoll", "rocket", "tiny" }

        local e = x:FindFirstChild("AdminPanel")
        if e then
            local Q, V = pcall(function()
                local O = e.AdminPanel.Content.ScrollingFrame:FindFirstChild(J)
                local e = O and O:FindFirstChild("Timer")
                return e and e.Visible
            end)
            if Q and V then
                return true
            end
        end
        if type(lastActionUse) == "table" then
            local e = lastActionUse[J]
            local Q = ACTION_COOLDOWNS and ACTION_COOLDOWNS[J] or 0
            if e and Q > 0 and tick() - e < Q then
                return true
            end
        end
        return false
    end
    local function J(e)
        if type(lastActionUse) == "table" then
            lastActionUse[e] = tick()
        end
        if P.AdminButtonCache and P.AdminButtonCache[e] then
            for Q, Q in ipairs(P.AdminButtonCache[e]) do
                if Q and Q.Parent then
                    pcall(function()
                        Q.BackgroundColor3 = W.Error
                    end)
                    task.delay(ACTION_COOLDOWNS and ACTION_COOLDOWNS[e] or 5, function()
                        if Q and Q.Parent then
                            pcall(function()
                                local V = e == "balloon"
                                    and P.BalloonedPlayers
                                    and next(P.BalloonedPlayers) ~= nil
                                Q.BackgroundColor3 = V and W.Error or W.SurfaceHighlight
                            end)
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local function e()
        local Q = P.BalloonedPlayers and next(P.BalloonedPlayers) ~= nil
        if P.AdminButtonCache and P.AdminButtonCache.balloon then
            for V, V in ipairs(P.AdminButtonCache.balloon) do
                if V and V.Parent then
                    pcall(function()
                        V.BackgroundColor3 = Q and W.Error or W.SurfaceHighlight
                    end)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local function Q()
        local V = { "ragdoll", "balloon", "rocket", "jail" }

        for q, q in ipairs(V) do
            O[q] = true

                return q

        end

            if not O[V] and not f(V) then

    end
    local function V(O, q)
        q = tonumber(q) or 0.1

                task.delay(c * q, function()
                    if not O or not O.Parent or O == C then
                        return
                    end
                    local q, B = pcall(_G.runAdminCommand, O, z)
                    if q and B then
                        J(z)
                        if z == "balloon" then
                            P.BalloonedPlayers[O.UserId] = true
                            e()
                        end
                    end
                end)
                c = c + 1
            end
        end
    end
    local O = 0
    local function q()
        local c = {}
        local z = C.Character
        local B = z and z:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not B then
            return c
        end
        local z = tonumber(i.ProximityRange) or 15
        for Y, Y in ipairs(r:GetPlayers()) do
            if Y ~= C and Y.Parent and Y.Character and Y.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                local I = Y.Character.HumanoidRootPart
                local p = (I.Position - B.Position).Magnitude
                if p <= z then
                    if
                        not (
                            i.DisableProximitySpamOnMoby and y(Y)
                            or i.DisableProximitySpamOnKawaifu and K(Y)
                        )
                    then
                        table.insert(c, Y)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        table.sort(c, function(z, B)
            return z.UserId < B.UserId
        end)
        return c
    end
    local function c()
        local z = q()
        if #z <= 0 then
            return
        end
        local q = Q()
        if not q then
            return
        end
        O = O % #z + 1
        local B = z[O]
        if not B then
            return
        end
        local O, z = pcall(_G.runAdminCommand, B, q)
        if O and z then
            J(q)
            if q == "balloon" then
                P.BalloonedPlayers[B.UserId] = true
                e()
            end
        end
    end
    local function O(q, z, B, Y)
        local I = Vector3.new(Y / 2, Y / 2, Y / 2)
        local Y = B - I
        local p = B + I
        local function B(I, l)
            if math.abs(l) < 1e-8 then
                if I >= 0 then
                    return math.huge
                else
                    return -math.huge
                end
            end
            return I / l
        end
        local I = B(Y.X - q.X, z.X)
        local l = B(p.X - q.X, z.X)
        if I > l then
            I, l = l, I
        end
        local W = B(Y.Y - q.Y, z.Y)
        local X = B(p.Y - q.Y, z.Y)
        if W > X then
            W, X = X, W
        end
        if I > X or W > l then
            return false
        end
        if W > I then
            I = W
        end
        if X < l then
            l = X
        end
        local W = B(Y.Z - q.Z, z.Z)
        local Y = B(p.Z - q.Z, z.Z)
        if W > Y then
            W, Y = Y, W
        end
        if I > Y or W > l then
            return false
        end
        return true
    end
    R.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if i.ClickToAP then
            local q = a.CurrentCamera
            local z = j:GetMouseLocation()
            local B = q:ViewportPointToRay(z.X, z.Y)
            local q = nil
            local z = math.huge
            for Y, Y in ipairs(r:GetPlayers()) do
                if Y ~= C and Y.Character and Y.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and Y.Parent then
                    local I = Y.Character.HumanoidRootPart
                    local p = I.Position
                    if O(B.Origin, B.Direction, p, 8) then
                        local I = (B.Origin - p).Magnitude
                        if I < z then
                            z = I
                            q = Y
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            n.Adornee = q and q.Character or nil
        else
            n.Adornee = nil
        end
    end)
    j.InputBegan:Connect(function(n, q)
        if q then
            return
        end
        if n.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            return
        end
        if not i.ClickToAP then
            return
        end
        local n = a.CurrentCamera
        local q = j:GetMouseLocation()
        local z = n:ViewportPointToRay(q.X, q.Y)
        local n = nil
        local q = math.huge
        for B, B in ipairs(r:GetPlayers()) do
            if B ~= C and B.Character and B.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and B.Parent then
                local Y = B.Character.HumanoidRootPart
                local I = Y.Position
                if O(z.Origin, z.Direction, I, 8) then
                    local O = (z.Origin - I).Magnitude
                    if O < q then
                        q = O
                        n = B
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if not n then
            return
        end
        if i.DisableClickToAPOnMoby and y(n) then
            F("CLICK TO AP", "Disabled on Moby users")
            return
        end
        if i.DisableClickToAPOnKawaifu and K(n) then
            F("CLICK TO AP", "Disabled on Kawaifu users")
            return
        end
        local O = false
        for q, q in ipairs(d) do
            if not f(q) then
                O = true
                break
            end
        end
        if O then
            if i.ClickToAPSingleCommand then
                local d = Q()
                if d then
                    local f, Q = pcall(_G.runAdminCommand, n, d)
                    if f and Q then
                        J(d)
                        if d == "balloon" then
                            P.BalloonedPlayers[n.UserId] = true
                            e()
                        end
                        F("CLICK AP", "Sent " .. d .. " to " .. n.Name)
                    else
                        F("CLICK AP", "Failed to send " .. d .. " to " .. n.Name)
                    end
                else
                    F("CLICK AP", "All commands on cooldown")
                end
            else
                V(n)
                F("CLICK AP", "Triggered on " .. n.Name)
            end
        else
            local d = x:WaitForChild("AdminPanel", 5)
            if d then
                local f = d:WaitForChild("AdminPanel"):WaitForChild("Profiles"):WaitForChild("ScrollingFrame")
                local d = f:FindFirstChild(n.Name)
                if d then
                    fireClick(d)
                    F("CLICK AP", "Selected " .. n.Name)
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    local d = false
    local function n()
        if d then
            return
        end
        d = true
        task.spawn(function()
            while ProximityAPActive do
                c()
                task.wait(0.1)
            end
            d = false
        end)
    end
    local d = 0
    R.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local R = os.clock()
        if R - d < 0.2 then
            return
        end
        d = R
        if ProximityAPActive then
            n()
        end
    end)
end
local function R()
    local function d(n)
        local f, J = pcall(function()
            return string.lower(tostring(n.Text or ""))
        end)
        return f and J or ""
    end
    local function n(f)
        if not f then
            return false
        end
        local J = false
        local e = false
        local Q = false
        local V = 0
        for O, O in ipairs(f:GetDescendants()) do
            if O:IsA("TextLabel") or O:IsA("TextButton") then
                local f = d(O)
                if f:find("admin panel", 1, true) then
                    J = true
                end
                if f:find("player list", 1, true) then
                    e = true
                end
                if f == "tp" then
                    Q = true
                end
                if f == "â" or f == "ð" or f == "ð" or f == "ð" then
                    V += 1
                end
            end
        end
        return J and e and V >= 4 and not Q
    end
    local function d(f)
        if not f or not f:IsA("ScreenGui") then
            return false
        end
        if f.Name == "XiAdminPanel" then
            return true
        end
        if n(f) then
            return true
        end
        return false
    end
    local n = {}
    pcall(function()
        table.insert(n, x)
    end)
    pcall(function()
        local f = game:GetService("CoreGui")
        if f then
            table.insert(n, f)
        end
    end)
    for f, f in ipairs(n) do
        for n, n in ipairs(f:GetChildren()) do
            if d(n) then
                pcall(function()
                    n:Destroy()
                end)
            end
        end
    end
end

    end
    local function U(o, T, j)
        local t = T == "kawaifu" and "KAWAIFU" or "MOBY"
        local G = T == "kawaifu" and Color3.fromRGB(228, 91, 176) or Color3.fromRGB(180, 50, 50)
        local v = T == "kawaifu" and 58 or 38
        local T = o:FindFirstChild("UserTag_MOBY") or o:FindFirstChild("UserTag_KAWAIFU")
        if T then
            T:Destroy()
        end
        local T = J(
            "TextLabel",
            {
                Name = "UserTag_" .. t,
                BackgroundColor3 = G,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                Position = UDim2.fromOffset(j, 8),
                Size = UDim2.fromOffset(v, 16),
                Text = t,
                Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
                TextSize = 9,
                TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
                ZIndex = 11,
                Parent = o,
            }
        )
        e(T, 5)
        return T
    end
    local function o(T)
        if K and K(T) then
            return "kawaifu"
        end
        if y and y(T) then
            return "moby"
        end
        return nil
    end
    local function y(K)
        local T = z[K.UserId]
        if not T or not T.Parent then
            return
        end
        l(K)
        local j = T:FindFirstChild("UserTag_MOBY") or T:FindFirstChild("UserTag_KAWAIFU")
        local t = o(K)
        if not t then
            if j then
                j:Destroy()
            end
            return
        end
        local function G(v)
            local M = v and v:FindFirstChild("DisplayNameLabel")
            local v = 90
            if M and M:IsA("TextLabel") then
                local w = M.TextBounds.X
                if w and w > 0 then
                    v = w
                end
            end
            return 50 + v + 8
        end
        local v = t == "kawaifu" and "UserTag_KAWAIFU" or "UserTag_MOBY"
        local M = G(T)
        if j and j.Name == v then
            j.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(M, 8)
        else
            if j then
                j:Destroy()
            end
            U(T, t, M)
        end
        task.defer(function()
            local T = z[K.UserId]
            if not T or not T.Parent then
                return
            end
            local j = o(K)
            local K = T:FindFirstChild("UserTag_MOBY") or T:FindFirstChild("UserTag_KAWAIFU")
            if not j then
                if K then
                    K:Destroy()
                end
                return
            end
            local o = j == "kawaifu" and "UserTag_KAWAIFU" or "UserTag_MOBY"
            local t = G(T)
            if K and K.Name == o then
                K.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(t, 8)
            else
                if K then
                    K:Destroy()
                end
                U(T, j, t)
            end
        end)
    end
    local function U(K, o, T, j)
        local t = J(
            "TextButton",
            {
                Size = UDim2.fromOffset(R.ButtonSize, R.ButtonSize),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                Text = "",
                LayoutOrder = T,
                Parent = K,
            }
        )
        local K = J(
            "Frame",
            {
                Name = "Box",
                AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
                Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0),
                Size = UDim2.fromOffset(R.BoxSize, R.BoxSize),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                Parent = t,
            }
        )
        e(K, 6)
        local T = J(
            "Frame",
            {
                Name = "Fill",
                BackgroundColor3 = R.ButtonBg,
                BackgroundTransparency = 0.08,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
                Parent = K,
            }
        )
        e(T, 6)
        local T = Q(K, R.ButtonStroke, 1.1, 1)
        T.Name = "Stroke"
        J(
            "TextLabel",
            {
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
                Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0),
                Size = UDim2.fromOffset(R.BoxSize, R.BoxSize),
                Text = o.label,
                Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
                TextSize = R.IconSize,
                TextColor3 = R.Text,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center,
                TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center,
                Parent = t,
            }
        )
        t:SetAttribute("Locked", false)
        t:SetAttribute("ActionName", o.action)
        t:SetAttribute("TargetPlayerName", j and j.Name or "")
        Y(t, "normal")
        t.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            if t:GetAttribute("Locked") then
                return
            end
            Y(t, "hover")
        end)
        t.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            if t:GetAttribute("Locked") then
                return
            end
            Y(t, "normal")
        end)
        table.insert(q[o.action], t)
        local K = f(o.action)
        local q = p(j)
        local T = K or q
        t.Active = not T
        t:SetAttribute("Locked", T)
        if T then
            Y(t, "locked")
        end
        t.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if p(j) then
                F("ADMIN", "AP Panel blocked on Moby users")
                l(j)
                return
            end
            if o.action == "tp" then
                local K, q = X(j)
                if not K then
                    F("ADMIN", "Failed TP: " .. tostring(q or (j and j.Name or "target")))
                    return
                end
                F("ADMIN", "TP'd to first floor of " .. (j and j.Name or "target"))
                return
            end
            if f(o.action) then
                return
            end
            local K = j and j.Name or "target"
            F("ADMIN", "Attempting " .. o.action .. " on " .. K)
            W(o.action)
            local q = O(j, o.action)
            if q == false then
                F("ADMIN", "Failed to send " .. o.action .. " to " .. K)
            else
                F("ADMIN", "Sent " .. o.action .. " to " .. K)
            end
        end)
        return t
    end
    local function K(q)
        if not q or q == C then
            return
        end
        if z[q.UserId] and z[q.UserId].Parent then
            return
        end
        local o = 0
        for Y, Y in ipairs(c.listHolder:GetChildren()) do
            if Y:IsA("Frame") and Y.Name:match("^Row_") then
                o = o + 1
            end
        end
        local Y = o % 2 == 0
        local o = Y and R.RowA or R.RowB
        local Y = J(
            "Frame",
            {
                Name = ("Row_%d"):format(q.UserId),
                BackgroundColor3 = o,
                BackgroundTransparency = 0.4,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, R.RowHeight),
                ZIndex = 5,
                Parent = c.listHolder,
            }
        )
        B = B + 1
        Y:SetAttribute("CreateIndex", B)
        Y:SetAttribute("PlayerName", q.Name)
        e(Y, R.RowCorner)
        Q(Y, R.AccentSoft, 1, 0.98)
        Y.ClipsDescendants = true
        z[q.UserId] = Y
        Y.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            Y.BackgroundColor3 = R.RowHover
        end)
        Y.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            Y.BackgroundColor3 = o
        end)
        local B = J(
            "Frame",
            {
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(9, 10, 14),
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                Size = UDim2.fromOffset(34, 34),
                Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 10),
                Parent = Y,
            }
        )
        e(B, 8)
        Q(B, R.AccentSoft, 1, 0.72)
        local Q = J(
            "ImageLabel",
            {
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
                Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
                Image = "rbxthumb://type=AvatarHeadShot&id=" .. q.UserId .. "&w=150&h=150",
                ZIndex = 10,
                Parent = B,
            }
        )
        e(Q, 8)
        local e = -(R.ActionsWidth + R.SafeGap)
        local Q = J(
            "TextLabel",
            {
                Name = "DisplayNameLabel",
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Position = UDim2.fromOffset(50, 6),
                Size = UDim2.new(1, e, 0, 20),
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                Text = q.DisplayName,
                Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
                TextSize = R.NameSize,
                TextColor3 = R.Text,
                ZIndex = 10,
                Parent = Y,
            }
        )
        y(q)
        J(
            "TextLabel",
            {
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Position = UDim2.fromOffset(50, 23),
                Size = UDim2.new(1, e, 0, 16),
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                Text = "@" .. q.Name,
                Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium,
                TextSize = R.UserSize,
                TextColor3 = R.SubText,
                ZIndex = 10,
                Parent = Y,
            }
        )
        local Q = J(
            "TextLabel",
            {
                Name = "Status",
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Position = UDim2.fromOffset(50, 37),
                Size = UDim2.new(1, e, 0, 14),
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                Text = "",
                Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
                TextSize = R.StatusSize,
                TextColor3 = R.Yellow,
                ZIndex = 10,
                Parent = Y,
            }
        )
        x[q.UserId] = Q
        local e = J(
            "Frame",
            {
                Name = "Actions",
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
                Position = UDim2.new(1, -8.0, 0.5, 0),
                Size = UDim2.fromOffset(R.ActionsWidth, R.ButtonSize),
                ZIndex = 12,
                Parent = Y,
            }
        )
        local Q = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        Q.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
        Q.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        Q.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left
        Q.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
        Q.Padding = UDim.new(0, 0)
        Q.Parent = e
        for Q, B in ipairs(n) do
            U(e, B, Q, q)
        end
        local n = J(
            "Frame",
            {
                Name = "Line",
                BackgroundColor3 = R.RowLine,
                BackgroundTransparency = 0.93,
                BorderSizePixel = 0,
                Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 1, -1.0),
                Size = UDim2.new(1, -32.0, 0, 1),
                Parent = Y,
            }
        )
        Y.InputBegan:Connect(function(R)
            if R.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                return
            end
            if p(q) then
                F("ADMIN", "AP Panel blocked on Moby users")
                l(q)
                return
            end
            local R = { "ragdoll", "jail", "rocket", "balloon", "jumpscare", "inverse", "tiny", "morph" }
            local n = false
            for J, J in ipairs(R) do
                if not f(J) then
                    n = true
                    break
                end
            end
            if n then
                local n = false
                for J, J in ipairs(R) do
                    if f(J) then
                        continue
                    end
                    W(J)
                    if p(q) then
                        F("ADMIN", "AP Panel blocked on Moby users")
                        l(q)
                        break
                    end
                    O(q, J)
                    n = true
                    task.wait(0.01)
                end
                if n then
                    F("ADMIN", "Triggered ALL on " .. q.Name)
                end
            end
        end)
        local R = {}
        table.insert(
            R,
            q:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Stealing"):Connect(function()
                a(q)
            end)
        )
        table.insert(
            R,
            q:GetAttributeChangedSignal("StealingIndex"):Connect(function()
                a(q)
            end)
        )
        table.insert(
            R,
            q.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(n)
                task.defer(function()
                    y(q)
                    local f = pcall(function()
                        n.DescendantAdded:Connect(function()
                            task.defer(function()
                                y(q)
                            end)
                        end)
                        n.DescendantRemoving:Connect(function()
                            task.defer(function()
                                y(q)
                            end)
                        end)
                    end)
                end)
            end)
        )
        if q.Character then
            table.insert(
                R,
                q.Character.DescendantAdded:Connect(function()
                    task.defer(function()
                        y(q)
                    end)
                end)
            )
            table.insert(
                R,
                q.Character.DescendantRemoving:Connect(function()
                    task.defer(function()
                        y(q)
                    end)
                end)
            )
        end
        V[q.UserId] = R
        a(q)
        y(q)
        l(q)
        P()
    end
    local function R(n)
        local f = z[n.UserId]
        if f then
            f:Destroy()
            z[n.UserId] = nil
        end
        x[n.UserId] = nil
        local f = V[n.UserId]
        if f then
            for J, J in ipairs(f) do
                pcall(function()
                    J:Disconnect()
                end)
            end
            V[n.UserId] = nil
        end
    end
    c.refreshBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        for n, n in ipairs(r:GetPlayers()) do
            if n ~= C then
                K(n)
                a(n)
            end
        end
        P()
        F("ADMIN PANEL", "Player list refreshed")
    end)
    for P, P in ipairs(r:GetPlayers()) do
        if P ~= C then
            K(P)
        end
    end
    r.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(P)
        task.defer(function()
            K(P)
        end)
    end)
    r.PlayerRemoving:Connect(R)
    task.spawn(function()
        while c.adminGui.Parent do
            I()
            for P, P in ipairs(r:GetPlayers()) do
                if P ~= C then
                    y(P)
                end
            end
            task.wait(0.25)
        end
    end)
    if i.HideAdminPanel then
        c.adminGui.Enabled = false
    end
end
d()
